**Blockers:** Capture quality: glare, focus and skew from a phone on a stand under room lighting may degrade Textract results.; WebSocket support on Vega is unverified (WebFetch summary only).; Language coverage: Indian bills often mix scripts, and Textract's support for Hindi is not verified, so the MVP is English-only.; Liability perception around financial and medical documents. The app must stay read-only and say clearly that it can be wrong.; Crowded accessibility theme on Fire TV (8+ audio-description repos and a 'vision assistant' entrant).

**Reasons judges might reject it:** Fire TV accessibility entries are crowded, and judges may lump this in with audio-description projects.; It may be seen as an OCR wrapper if the grounding and the remote-as-shutter are not shown clearly.; It needs a phone, so it is not literally remote-only, and setting up a phone stand may seem unrealistic for the target user.; Sending sensitive documents to the cloud is a privacy concern, made sharper by the 2026 privacy backlash.; Errors in financial or medical readings would carry liability, and demo evidence of safety may feel thin.; The demo may use simulated low vision rather than real users.

**Gate result:** overall **pass** (eligibility pass, feasibility pass; native overlap medium, visible crowding medium).

- Eligibility: A Vega OS app on the VVD meets the Fire TV rule (Rules L152). It deliberately avoids the infeasible TV camera (no Vega camera API or camera hardware, firetv.md §7) by using a phone on a stand as the sensor. The TV handles remote keys, rendering, Polly audio playback and VoiceView labels. It needs no TV microphone and no arbitrary Alexa voice. Demo documents are the team's own and redacted, or synthetic and labelled, so there are no third-party IP issues.
- Feasibility: The 115 h solo estimate fits within the roughly 117 h capacity, but with only about 2 h of buffer; both figures are estimates. Textract AnalyzeDocument QUERIES+LAYOUT (synchronous, with geometry and confidence) is verified (.firecrawl/idea-ftv-textract-analyzedocument.md), and Polly is verified. The unverified pieces each have a rules-safe fallback: Vega WebSocket (fall back to auto-capture plus polling), Textract accuracy on photos taken by a phone on a stand (fall back to DetectDocumentText plus cited lines), and Bedrock quota (the core runs on Textract and deterministic rendering without Nova). One person can record the demo. The Fire OS fallback needs a Fire OS stick or AQC Live Device …
- Conditions: (1) Textract QUERIES field accuracy of about 90% or better on the team's own phone-on-stand captures under living-room light, which is the concept's own kill threshold. (2) Demo documents are team-owned and redacted, or synthetic and labelled. Account numbers are masked on screen. Read-only scope only, with no payment or medication advice. (3) English-only MVP, since Textract support for Hindi was not verified. (4) For a real-device take: a Vega 4K Select on OS 1.2 via `vega devmode`, or a Fire OS build on a Fire OS stick or AQC Live Device Interaction.
- Native overlap: Amazon's own vision accessibility includes Echo Show 'Show and Tell', which identifies items you hold up, and Alexa+ 'Visual Input', which uses the Echo Show camera 'to identify objects and assist with daily tasks' (Amazon help page, undated, retrieved 2026-10-03). The page does not say whether either reads letters or bills or pulls out due dates and amounts. Fire TV and Echo Screen Magnifier and VoiceView only magnify on-screen UI. Non-Amazon incumbents are dedicated CCTV magnifiers at $400–$4…
- Crowding: The accessibility cluster is the most crowded Fire TV theme: about 12 visible blind/low-vision repos across tracks, at least 8 of them Fire TV AI audio description (github.md theme counts). vikrant-29/ai-tv-vision-assistant ('D-pad-first AI vision assistant designed for Fire TV', Fire OS, Flutter) has unknown scope and is a possible overlap. No visible entrant reads physical documents on the TV. The risk is that judges lump it in with the audio-description entries.
- Day-one proof: End to end: an OK key press on the VVD sends 'capture' over WSS or polling. The phone kiosk page on a stand grabs a full-resolution still and PUTs it to S3. Textract AnalyzeDocument QUERIES returns sender, amount and due date with bounding boxes for 10 of the team's own redacted bills, with at least 9 of 10 fields correct. The VVD then renders the reflowed card and a zoomed evidence crop at 60–96 px.
- Redesign to pass: Not required. Recommended: open the video with the contrast to Echo Show Visual Input (10-foot reflow, evidence crops a viewer can check, remote-only control), and keep the confidence-threshold 'Not sure, show me' state visible.

**Round-2 judge ratings (1–5; base case: solo, India, VVD on Mac, no Bee hardware, no US Ring device). Weighted = 0.25·Tech + 0.20·Design + 0.20·Impact + 0.20·Originality + 0.15·Feasibility.**

| Judge lens | Tech | Design | Impact | Originality | Feasibility | Weighted | Confidence | One-line verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| devrel | 4 | 4 | 3 | 3 | 3 | 3.45 | medium | Still a solid, fully-real Fire TV accessibility entry after the redesign, but its novelty narrowed to the evidence-checked card. |
| engineer | 4 | 4 | 3 | 3 | 3 | 3.45 | high | The strongest real-tech Fire TV option: a deterministic, checkable Textract loop on a genuine Vega app, narrowed honestly after Apple Magnifier, with a thin solo buffer. |
| product | 4 | 5 | 4 | 3 | 3 | 3.85 | high | Redesigned Mail Check is the best-designed, most credible Fire TV entry here; originality is narrowed by Apple and free phone readers, so the video must lead with evidence zoom and remote-only capture. |
| **Mean of three** | 4.00 | 4.33 | 3.33 | 3.00 | 3.00 | **3.58** | | Scenario weighted: solo+hw 3.58; duo 3.73; duo+hw 3.73 |

**Why each judge rated it that way (verbatim rationale, one per criterion):**

- *devrel* — Tech: Scored as BigPrint Mail Check (prior_redteam FTV-3.redesign): remote-only capture over WSS, Textract AnalyzeDocument QUERIES with confidence gate and geometry zoom, KeplerA11y caption styles (JDR-10), Polly speech marks; no model on the critical path and fully real on the VVD. Not 5: WebSocket on VVD untested (JDR-11) and Textract accuracy on phone-on-stand photos unmeasured. — Design: Remote-only journey, 'Not sure, show me' evidence zoom, masked numbers, matches Amazon's Fire TV accessibility direction (IDEATION_CONTEXT). Held back by the one-time phone-stand setup and because reflow/read-aloud are no longer novel (Apple Magnifier Reader Mode, JDR-28). — Impact: Low-vision elders checking mail is specific and frequent (CDC ~7M VI per prior evidence), but free first-party substitutes now cover reflow and read-aloud (red team), so the incremental value is the verifiable fact card. — Originality: Red team revised to 3: evidence-zoom plus confidence gate is the wedge; the magnifier half collapsed against Apple/Seeing AI/Lookout; blind/low-vision is the most crowded theme (~12 repos) though those are audio description. — Feasibility: ~95-100 h re-estimate fits ~104 h with little buffer, and the day-2 Textract kill gate (9/10 fields) can end the concept.
  - Scenario adjustments: solo+hw tech 4, feasibility 3 (Real 4K Select take possible on OS 1.2; cap it at a few hours.); duo tech 4, feasibility 4 (~70 h each; helper-share stretch returns.); duo+hw tech 4, feasibility 4 (As duo plus real-device footage.)
- *engineer* — Tech: Scored as the red-team redesign 'BigPrint Mail Check' (prior_redteam.json FTV-3.redesign). The required tech is load-bearing and honest: a Vega RN app on the VVD takes an OK press, sends a WSS command (HTTPS long-poll pre-built as fallback) to a fixed Android Chrome phone that captures a full-resolution still via ImageCapture.takePhoto, and Textract AnalyzeDocument QUERIES+LAYOUT returns answers with Confidence and … — Design: The TV supplies the large high-contrast display this user lacks and the remote is a sight-free tactile controller, matching Amazon's remote-only and VoiceView guidance (IDEATION_CONTEXT). D-pad line stepping bound to KeplerA11y caption styles (getters verified in ftv2-kepler-a11y-readme.md), Polly sentence marks, masked digits, and the evidence zoom make a complete flow. Deductions: a phone on a stand plus a placeme… — Impact: Credible population (CDC about 7M with vision impairment, PMC and NCOA figures in the Oct 3 digests) and an established paid category (AFB CCTV magnifiers $1,800-4,000; DaVinci Pro HD OCR $4,998 per rival matrix). Cut from the prior 4 because free first-party rivals now cover the core loop (Apple Magnifier Reader Mode, Seeing AI, Lookout; red team), leaving remote-only control and verifiable fact cards as the wedge,… — Originality: No visible Fire TV entrant reads physical documents on the TV (27 repos in gh2-firetv-hackathon.json; accessibility entries there are audio description: described, sema-firetv, narratv, gap-track). The remote-as-shutter across devices and geometry-grounded evidence crops are fresh, but the magnifier-plus-reflow half is no longer novel (Apple, May 2025) and 'what does this letter say' is a latent multimodal-LLM capab… — Feasibility: Red-team re-estimate 95-100 h against about 104 h (estimates) leaves 4-9 h of buffer for six surfaces (Vega app, Android kiosk, backend of Textract/Polly/S3/KMS/DynamoDB/API GW, 10-document evaluation, OSS viewer, video). One person can shoot the demo; Textract and Polly are verified; Bedrock is off the path. Tight but completable, with the day-2 kill gate (9 of 10 QUERY fields correct) as the real risk.
  - Scenario adjustments: solo+hw tech 4, feasibility 3 (A real 4K Select take restores the 10-foot premise the demo attack says a Mac window loses, but only if the stick is on OS 1.2 (listed 1.1) and dev mode unlocks (community report). Hours unchanged.); duo tech 4, feasibility 4 (At about 70 h each (estimate) the evaluation, OSS viewer and the cut helper-share can all land with buffer.); duo+hw tech 4, feasibility 4 (As duo_no_hw plus an optional real-device take.)
- *product* — Tech: Scored as the red-team redesign 'BigPrint Mail Check' (prior_redteam.json FTV-3.redesign): remote-as-shutter over WSS to a fixed Android phone, Textract AnalyzeDocument QUERY_RESULT with Confidence and Geometry, deterministic card, TV reflow with D-pad line stepping bound to KeplerA11ySettings caption styles, Polly speech marks, Bedrock off the critical path. The Vega app is load-bearing as display and sole control … — Design: The most complete device-appropriate design in the set: remote-only capture, 60-96 px reflow in the viewer's high-contrast colours, line stepping, 'Show me' evidence zoom to the printed region, a 'Not sure, show me' confidence state instead of a guess, masked account numbers, sentence read-aloud, and system caption-style binding (redesign items 1-4). This is exactly the Blueprint's remote-only, visible-focus, respec… — Impact: Daily, specific need: reading mail and bills with low vision; about 7 million US adults have vision impairment incl. 1 million blind (CDC, rng2-vision-prevalence.json), and today's answers are $1,800-5,000 desktop CCTV magnifiers or hand-held phone apps (rival matrix). Credible audience in Fire TV and Android households. Not 5 because Apple Magnifier for Mac Reader Mode (riv2-apple-magnifier-mac-support.json) and fr… — Originality: Red team: the magnifier half collapses against Apple Magnifier for Mac with Accessibility Reader and free phone readers; the fact-card half is latent multimodal-LLM capability; Fire TV Miracast can mirror any Android magnifier (riv2-vega-mirroring.json). What survives and is not verified in any rival: remote-only capture with a never-touched phone, and non-generative facts shown beside a geometry crop with a confide… — Feasibility: Re-estimated 95-100 h solo leaves about 5-10 h buffer (redesign; estimate). Day-2 kill gates: 9 of 10 QUERY fields correct on stand captures, p50 OK-to-card latency (6-12 s plausible), WSS or long-poll on the VVD. Android-Chrome-only kiosk (ImageCapture.takePhoto unsupported on iOS Safari). One person can record the demo.
  - Scenario adjustments: solo+hw tech 4, feasibility 3 (A 4K Select take with the tactile remote strengthens the ten-foot story but the VVD path already shows the Vega app doing the work; OS 1.2 check required.); duo tech 4, feasibility 4 (70 h per person (prior estimate) restores buffer for the 30-document evaluation and the helper-share stretch.); duo+hw tech 4, feasibility 4 (Split build plus optional real-device footage; tech stays 4 because capture remains phone-side.)

**Round-1 judge ratings (3 Oct, same model; shown for traceability):** devrel 4/4/4/4/3 = 3.85; engineer 4/4/4/4/3 = 3.85; product 4/4/4/4/3 = 3.85

### Track: Alexa+

#### ALX-1: CarpoolMend: cross-family carpool driver-gap repair

**Round:** 1. **Track:** Alexa+ (primary); AWS Builder (mini)

**Pitch:** When a parent drops a carpool turn, CarpoolMend filters drivers who cannot actually cover it (seats, boosters, arrival window), ranks the rest by a fairness ledger, asks ONE family at a time to accept, and only after acceptance writes every family's calendar with idempotent, compensable writes.

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| Primary user | Parents in recurring 3-6 family school/sports carpools (US-first; India school/tuition runs plausible - inference). |
| Frequency | Inference: weekly in active carpools. GoKid vendor-hosted testimonial says schedules change daily (anecdote). No independent frequency data found. |
| Severity | Per incident high (child stranded - Maple vendor opinion), otherwise moderate coordination cost. Not quantified. |
| Current workaround | Group-text broadcast 'who can drive?'; GoKid notifies the whole carpool and parents volunteer for slots (its FAQ: 'does not auto-assign drivers'); spreadsheets. |
| Amazon integration and its indispensable role | Kitchen Echo Show is where 'I can't drive Thursday' is said; the requesting family's Alexa+ calls MCP tools; the targeted family hears/sees an acceptance card and answers by voice. All Alexa+ surfaces are simulated in our host. |
| Removal test (honest) | Honest: the engine is host-agnostic. Without Alexa+ it is a smarter GoKid (constraint-ranked targeted offers instead of broadcast). Alexa+ adds the ambient trigger and hands-free acceptance, not the core value. Weakness flagged; fit rests on voice-first household entry, not exclusivity. |
| Wow moment under 45 s | Two simulated Echo Shows side by side. Parent A: 'I can't do Thursday hockey pickup.' Card ranks 3 candidates with reasons (Lee: 2 free seats, has booster, owed 1 drive; Patel excluded: car full). Only Lee's screen chimes; Lee says 'Yes, I'll take it.' Both calendars update; audit strip shows 4 writes with idempotency keys. A forced calendar failure on one family visibly triggers compensation and retry. |
| Original mechanism | Deterministic constraint filter (seat and booster counts, arrive-by with travel time, licensed adult, family availability) + drive-credit fairness ledger + sequential targeted offers implemented as Step Functions .waitForTaskToken tasks with timeout falling through to next candidate + saga writes using client-chosen event IDs so retries are idempotent and compensation is exact + per-family tenant isolation (other families see only time, kids, pickup point). |
| Simulator or real-data route (data route) | Voice/text in simulated host -> Strands agent (Nova Lite, parse only) -> MCP server on Lambda or AgentCore Runtime -> DynamoDB (groups, ledger, offers, idempotency keys) -> Step Functions saga -> Google Calendar API per family -> MCP Apps card + host notification pane. |
| Authentication | Per-family Google OAuth (test users); MCP endpoint behind Cognito JWT or bearer token, Origin validation, 127.0.0.1 binding locally; offer responses carry signed one-time tokens; no token passthrough. |
| Preview constraints | Alexa+ MCP Toolkit, CLI and Web Simulator are partner-gated; expected integration level is 'None whatsoever' (forum 45397). Cross-account Alexa notifications cannot be shown; must label host as simulated. |
| Hardware | Mac only. Optional phone to show a second family. |
| SDK versions | @modelcontextprotocol/sdk 1.32.0 or v2 server 2.3.0 (verify 2025-11-25 negotiation with Inspector 2.9.0); @modelcontextprotocol/ext-apps 2.0.3 (Node 20+); strands-agents 1.57.2; Node 20 LTS recommended over local Node 25. |
| Deployment prerequisites | AWS Paid-plan account (credits and AgentCore need it), Bedrock quota checked day 1 in us-east-1, Google Cloud OAuth consent screen in testing mode, endpoint kept live to Nov 20. |
| Firecrawl: research role | Research-time only: verified GoKid FAQ, App Store reviews and carpool-app landscape (.firecrawl/idea-alx-carpool.json). |
| Firecrawl: runtime role | none |
| AWS Builder path | Step Functions callback tokens ARE the acceptance protocol (wait, timeout, next candidate) and the saga compensator; DynamoDB conditional writes for idempotency; Lambda MCP host; Nova Lite only for utterance parsing; CloudWatch traces of each offer. |
| Open Source path | MIT repo plus an extracted 'offer-saga' package (state-machine definitions, constraint filter, fairness ledger, tests). Alternatively contribute the shared Alexa+ conformance harness described in ALX-2. |
| Fallback | Replace Google Calendar with per-family ICS feeds and Step Functions with an in-process state machine; keep the constraint/fairness engine and acceptance protocol. |
| Falsification test (≤1 day) | Within 1 day: ask 8-10 parents in active carpools (school/parent groups) about the last month; show a clickable mock of targeted offer vs broadcast. If fewer than half had a driver gap, or most prefer broadcast, the premise fails. In parallel replay 20 synthetic weeks: if broadcast-first reaches the same assignment in more than 80 percent of cases, the constraint ranking adds little. |
| Risk profile | Originality risk HIGH (crowded adjacent field); technical risk MEDIUM-HIGH (multi-tenant saga); platform risk LOW (fully simulated host). |
| Estimate (unmeasured) | Solo 100 h; two-person 68 h per person; cloud 5-20 (estimate). Assumptions: Estimates. ~6 productive h/day over 18 build days; reuse ext-apps basic-host for UI; Nova Lite at $0.06/$0.24 per M tokens (Ohio table) about $0.50 for 2,000 calls; Lambda/DynamoDB near free tier; Step Functions pricing not verified in reviewed sources, assumed under $2 at demo scale; hosting to Nov 20. |

**Demand evidence** (quality labelled by the researcher):

- 2026 systematic review (via Romper, 3 Aug 2026): parents report overcommunication and multi-platform burden. — [https://www.romper.com/parenting/the-hidden-back-to-school-supply-parents-cant-buy-more-bandwidth-the-rise-of-administrative-parenting](https://www.romper.com/parenting/the-hidden-back-to-school-supply-parents-cant-buy-more-bandwidth-the-rise-of-administrative-parenting) — *Journalism citing peer-reviewed review; medium*
- GoKid claims 2 million+ carpool trips scheduled; sells school licences $1,999-$7,999/yr. — [https://gokid.mobi/parents/](https://gokid.mobi/parents/) — *Vendor claim; low-medium*
- App Store reviewer asked GoKid for per-car seat capacity; developer replied it was planned (reply undated). — [https://apps.apple.com/us/app/gokid-carpool-organizer/id1050331956](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/gokid-carpool-organizer/id1050331956) — *Single user anecdote*
- Maple: a missed entry 'can mean a kid stranded after practice'; second-parent adoption predicts success. — [https://www.growmaple.com/blog-posts/best-family-calendar-app](https://www.growmaple.com/blog-posts/best-family-calendar-app) — *Competitor opinion; low*

**Rivals named at generation** (strongest feature; our difference; evidence status):

| Rival | Strongest feature | Our difference | Evidence status |
|---|---|---|---|
| [GoKid](https://gokid.mobi/faq/) | Free cross-platform carpool app: recurring schedules, volunteer-to-drive slots, whole-carpool notification when a driver is needed, live tracking (premium). | Targeted, constraint-checked offer with explicit acceptance and fairness ledger instead of broadcast; seat-capacity matching was a user request (status not verified in reviewed sources). | FAQ and App Store page read 2026-10-03 |
| [Carpool Kids](https://ptaourchildren.org/the-best-carpool-apps-for-parents/) | Five-star rating with 8k+ App Store reviews (per PTA magazine, Sep 2025); push updates and schedule sync. | Repair-on-change with acceptance and rollback; not verified in reviewed sources whether Carpool Kids does this. | Third-party article only; product page not read |
| [Alexa+ native calendar](https://www.aboutamazon.com/news/devices/new-alexa-top-features) | Family calendars, 'schedule events when everyone is free', per-person reminders. | Cross-household acceptance and multi-family write consistency; single-household scope inferred from Amazon pages. | Vendor page, updated 24 Jul 2026 |
| [Google CC (Labs)](https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/google-labs/cc-expanding-to-groups/) | Family agent that checks live drive times and keeps the calendar current as things change. | Cross-family accepted reassignment; not stated in CC post. | Official post read via digest |
| [KindHandoff (visible entrant)](https://github.com/shi1720/Amazon-Developer-Hackathon) | Repairs disrupted family support plans with helper acceptance and versioned handoffs. | Cross-household carpool domain, seat constraints, fairness ledger; mechanism overlap is real. | Repo description only |

**Smallest credible MVP:**

- MCP server (2025-11-25 Streamable HTTP, sessionful, accepts 2025-03-26/06-18): report_gap, list_candidates, send_offer, respond_offer, get_plan, undo_change
- Constraint filter + fairness ledger with unit tests on 20 synthetic weeks
- Step Functions offer/saga state machine with timeout and compensation
- Google Calendar writes for 3 test families; MCP Apps card for candidates and audit timeline
- Simulated two-device Alexa+-style host (Strands agent on Bedrock Nova Lite) clearly labelled SIMULATED

**Deliberately excluded stretch:**

- Live GPS tracking or child check-in
- Real SMS/WhatsApp delivery
- Route optimisation for multi-stop pickups
- Real Alexa+ cross-account notifications (gated)

**APIs, events and actions** (status as assessed by the researcher; 'unverified' means not confirmed in reviewed docs, 'gated' means not available to entrants, 'simulated' means not real in the demo):

| Status | API / event / action | Documentation |
|---|---|---|
| verified | MCP 2025-11-25 Streamable HTTP (Origin validation, MCP-Session-Id) | https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/2025-11-25/basic/transports |
| verified | MCP ToolAnnotations (destructiveHint, idempotentHint) | https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/2025-11-25/schema#toolannotations |
| verified | Step Functions Wait for Callback (.waitForTaskToken), SendTaskSuccess/SendTaskFailure | https://docs.aws.amazon.com/step-functions/latest/dg/connect-to-resource.html |
| unverified | Google Calendar API events.insert with client-supplied event id | https://developers.google.com/calendar/api/v3/reference/events/insert |
| verified | Strands Agents MCP client over Streamable HTTP | https://strandsagents.com/docs/user-guide/sdk/tools/mcp-tools/ |
| simulated | Alexa+ notifications / client lifecycle (cross-family acceptance prompt) | https://developer.amazon.com/docs/alexaplus/add-ons/mcp-addon-components-and-patterns.html |

**Blockers:** Visible crowding: 10+ Alexa+ household replanning/handoff repos incl. acceptance-based ones; Bedrock quota 0 or suspension reported on new accounts (forum 45416/45384); Multi-tenant OAuth plus saga is the largest build in the set

**Reasons judges might reject it:** Reads as an existing Alexa+ calendar feature or a crowded household-replanning idea; Alexa+ removal leaves essentially the same product; Cross-family push is simulated, weakening 'looks functional'; Consumer impact narrow (carpool households)

**Gate result:** overall **pass** (eligibility pass, feasibility pass; native overlap medium, visible crowding high).

- Eligibility: It uses the forum-accepted path: a self-hosted MCP server on spec 2025-11-25 over Streamable HTTP, plus a simulated Alexa+-style host labelled SIMULATED (Rules L154; forum 45058, 45403). It needs no gated toolkit, CLI or simulator. Cross-account Alexa notifications are shown as simulated and not claimed. Google Calendar is a third-party API used within its terms. The thesis is cross-household acceptance and reassignment, which is not a native Alexa+ feature in reviewed sources.
- Feasibility: The 100 h solo estimate fits the roughly 117 h capacity (estimate). Step Functions .waitForTaskToken, the MCP transport and annotations, and the Strands MCP client are verified. Google Calendar events.insert with a client-supplied ID is unverified. Multi-tenant OAuth plus a saga is the largest build in the set. Bedrock is used only for parsing, and forum 45058 accepts a deterministic simulation. Not verified in reviewed sources (general knowledge): in Google OAuth 'Testing' mode, refresh tokens expire after about 7 days. That would break a judge-facing demo through 20 Nov unless the ICS-feed fallback is used or the calendars are re-linked.
- Conditions: (1) The hosted MCP endpoint stays live through 20 Nov 12:00 PT (forum 45411), with a local-run path documented as well. (2) The host is labelled simulated. No claim of real Alexa+ deployment or cross-account push. (3) Calendar access stays valid through judging: either re-link the test families' Google OAuth before Stage 2, or switch to per-family ICS feeds. (4) Bedrock quota is checked on day 1, or the deterministic utterance parser is kept as the default.
- Native overlap: Alexa+ already does family calendars, 'schedule events when everyone is free' and person-targeted reminders, but within one household; single-household scope is inferred (aboutamazon, updated 24 Jul 2026). Google CC keeps a family calendar current. GoKid broadcasts driver needs. Targeted, constraint-checked cross-family offers with acceptance were not found natively. The concept's own removal test concedes that Alexa+ adds the entry point, not the core value.
- Crowding: Alexa+ is the most crowded track: about 158 visible repos, or about 53 per prize. At least 10 visible Alexa+ repos do household replanning or handoff, several with explicit acceptance: dev-pinegrass/homerelay ('explicit acceptance'), codeforcode111/relay-household, chowol0309/planpatch, ToukoUrsin/threshold, Elinfiny/HestiaRelay, BurpingSpider/readynest-alexaplus, Willyahaisibwe/household-coordinator-mcp, QIU-Guanzong/waypoint-local-planner, KoaChang/dinner-relay, and KindHandoff (cited by the …
- Day-one proof: The deployed MCP server (2025-11-25 Streamable HTTP, MCP-Session-Id, Origin validation) passes MCP Inspector tools/list. A Step Functions offer with .waitForTaskToken times out and falls through to the next candidate. A Google Calendar events.insert retry with the same client-chosen ID does not create a duplicate for a test family. Bedrock Nova Lite quota is non-zero, or the deterministic parser is in place.
- Redesign to pass: Not a gate fail. Originality is the exposure: put the carpool-specific constraint filter (seats, boosters, arrive-by) and the fairness ledger on screen in the first 20 s, and show the voice-first entry so the Alexa+ fit is not purely nominal.

**Round-2 judge ratings (1–5; base case: solo, India, VVD on Mac, no Bee hardware, no US Ring device). Weighted = 0.25·Tech + 0.20·Design + 0.20·Impact + 0.20·Originality + 0.15·Feasibility.**

| Judge lens | Tech | Design | Impact | Originality | Feasibility | Weighted | Confidence | One-line verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| devrel | 3 | 3 | 2 | 2 | 3 | 2.60 | medium | Technically honest but sits in the most crowded cluster of the most crowded track with thin impact evidence. |
| engineer | 4 | 3 | 2 | 2 | 3 | 2.85 | high | Impressive saga engineering in the most crowded theme of the most crowded track, with Alexa+ nominal and impact narrow. |
| product | 3 | 3 | 2 | 2 | 3 | 2.60 | high | Well-engineered saga over a crowded, low-stakes problem that Alexa+ and a dozen entrants already circle. |
| **Mean of three** | 3.33 | 3.00 | 2.00 | 2.00 | 3.00 | **2.68** | | Scenario weighted: solo+hw 2.68; duo 2.92; duo+hw 2.92 |

**Why each judge rated it that way (verbatim rationale, one per criterion):**

- *devrel* — Tech: MCP 2025-11-25 Streamable HTTP, Step Functions task-token acceptance and saga writes are substantive and hit 'agentic workflow across services'. Capped by the simulated host, multi-tenant Google OAuth as the largest build, and unverified calendar idempotency. — Design: Voice-first entry with acceptance cards is coherent, but the concept concedes Alexa+ adds only the entry point, and family calendar scheduling is a native Alexa+ feature (mcp.md §11), which forum 45397 says hurts Design (JDR-04). — Impact: Narrow US carpool segment; evidence is a GoKid vendor claim, one review and journalism; frequency inferred. — Originality: Household replanning with acceptance gates is a visible 13-repo cluster (KindHandoff, homerelay, HestiaRelay) in the most crowded track (~53 visible repos per prize). — Feasibility: 100 h vs ~104 h; OAuth and saga are the risk; hosting through 20 Nov required.
  - Scenario adjustments: solo+hw tech 3, feasibility 3 (No hardware changes anything; an Echo is optional.); duo tech 4, feasibility 4 (68 h each; saga and OAuth both land.); duo+hw tech 4, feasibility 4 (As duo.)
- *engineer* — Tech: Most sophisticated Alexa+ backend: a sessionful 2025-11-25 Streamable HTTP MCP server with ToolAnnotations, Step Functions .waitForTaskToken as the sequential acceptance protocol with timeout fall-through, saga writes keyed by client event IDs for exact compensation, DynamoDB conditional writes for idempotency, and a Strands MCP client host. Version negotiation must also accept 2025-03-26 because Amazon's own lifecy… — Design: Two simulated Echo Shows with targeted voice acceptance and an audit strip form a coherent voice-first flow, but the concept's own removal test concedes Alexa+ adds only the entry point, and cross-family notification is simulated, weakening forum 45058's 'look/feel functional' bar. — Impact: Narrow audience (recurring 3-6 family carpools); evidence is GoKid vendor claims, one App Store review and a competitor's opinion; per-incident severity is real but unquantified. — Originality: Household replanning and handoff is the most crowded visible Alexa+ theme (about 13 repos incl. homerelay, HestiaRelay, KindHandoff, threshold per the Oct 4 crowded list), and Alexa+ natively schedules 'when everyone is free' (rival matrix). The seat/booster constraint filter and fairness ledger are a thin wedge. — Feasibility: 100 h solo against about 104 h (estimates) with the largest Alexa+ build (multi-tenant OAuth plus saga plus host). The deterministic parser removes the Bedrock dependency; the ICS fallback removes OAuth expiry; the endpoint must stay live to 20 Nov (forum 45411). Fits, barely.
  - Scenario adjustments: solo+hw tech 4, feasibility 3 (No listed hardware touches a simulated Alexa+ host. No change.); duo tech 4, feasibility 4 (At about 68 h each (estimate) the saga, OAuth/ICS hardening and host fit with room.); duo+hw tech 4, feasibility 4 (Same as duo_no_hw.)
- *product* — Tech: Self-hosted MCP server (2025-11-25 Streamable HTTP) with Step Functions .waitForTaskToken offers, idempotent Google Calendar writes and a labelled simulated host (prior mechanism; gate day-one proof). Competent and verifiable in Inspector, but the Alexa+ layer is a simulation by necessity (IDEATION_CONTEXT 'expected Alexa+ integration level None whatsoever') and the engine is host-agnostic (removal test). — Design: Two simulated Echo Shows, ranked candidate cards with reasons, hands-free acceptance: voice-first and multimodal, but the cross-family push is simulated and the concept concedes Alexa+ adds only the ambient trigger (prior removal_test). Complete but generic household-coordination UI. — Impact: Carpool households are a narrow slice; GoKid claims 2 million+ trips (rival matrix) so a market exists, but the marginal gain (constraint-checked targeted offer plus fairness ledger over a group text) is small and unevidenced. Parent communication-overload evidence (trends_home.md T3 systematic review) is indirect. Alexa+ removal leaves the same product. — Originality: Household replanning with acceptance gates is a visible cluster of about 13 Alexa+ repos (refresh crowded themes: KindHandoff, homerelay, HestiaRelay, threshold) and Alexa+ natively does family calendars and 'when everyone is free' (rival matrix row 1; rivals_home.md finding 4). Google CC and Skylight/Hearth/Cozi cover the adjacent space. The seat/booster filter and fairness ledger are the only unverified-in-rivals … — Feasibility: 100 h solo vs about 104 h capacity (prior estimate; thin buffer); Google OAuth for test families must stay valid through 20 Nov or switch to ICS; hosted endpoint live through judging (gate conditions). No LLM required.
  - Scenario adjustments: solo+hw tech 3, feasibility 3 (Mac-only concept; the hardware set does not apply.); duo tech 3, feasibility 4 (68 h per person fits comfortably.); duo+hw tech 3, feasibility 4 (Same as duo without hardware.)

**Round-1 judge ratings (3 Oct, same model; shown for traceability):** devrel 3/3/2/2/3 = 2.60; engineer 4/3/2/2/3 = 2.85; product 3/3/2/2/3 = 2.60

**Red-team verdicts:** not red-teamed (did not reach the top of either round's ranking); ranking rests on gate and judge scores alone.

#### ALX-2: RideBook Access: paratransit trip booking add-on, built to Alexa+ certification requirements

**Round:** 1. **Track:** Alexa+ (primary); Open Source (mini) via conformance harness; AWS Builder optional

**Pitch:** An add-on a transit agency could publish so ADA paratransit riders book, change and cancel trips by voice instead of waiting on hold. It implements Amazon's published Functional Requirements literally: read-back confirmation, fare breakdown, receipts, duplicate-trip prevention, linked return-trip protection, resumable booking and stable IDs.

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| Primary user | ADA paratransit riders (people with disabilities incl. blind/low-vision, older adults) and caregivers booking for them; the publisher is the agency (fictional 'Lakeshore Access Transit' in the demo). |
| Frequency | Recurring: agencies require booking 1-14 days ahead or the day before, each leg separately (RTS, Pace, Valley Metro pages). Regular riders book several legs a week (inference). |
| Severity | High: FTA ADA compliance reviews confirmed long telephone holds are widespread (DREDF guide); late cancels count as no-shows and can lead to suspension (RTS points, Valley Metro 2-hour rule); missed medical trips (inference). |
| Current workaround | Phone reservation lines in limited hours; Via-powered RTS Plus app; Pace TripCheck to view/cancel; subscription trips for fixed schedules. |
| Amazon integration and its indispensable role | Voice-only Echo Dot is a first-class surface: certification section 9 (read back key details, explicit yes, max 5 options) maps one-to-one to this job; Echo Show gets Card/Carousel via MCP Apps; Alexa+ is the MCP client. Host simulated. |
| Removal test (honest) | Without Alexa+ the same MCP server could back a phone IVR or a ChatGPT app, and agencies already have apps (Via, TripCheck). What is lost is no-app, no-hold voice booking at home for riders who struggle with touch apps. Strongest Alexa+ fit of the three, but the backend is host-agnostic. |
| Wow moment under 45 s | Simulated Echo Dot, voice-only: 'Book a ride to Dr Rao tomorrow, arrive by 10.' -> '10 is full; I can get you there by 9:35 or 10:20.' -> rider picks -> read-back: 'Pickup 8:50 to 9:20 from home, wheelchair, PCA rides free, fare 3.25 from your Access balance, leaving 21.75. Book it?' 'Yes.' Receipt card. Rider says 'book it' again -> 'You already have trip LAT-4821.' Later: 'Cancel my morning ride' -> 'That is on time. Your 1:30 return is still booked - cancel it too?' |
| Original mechanism | Agency policy-as-config (horizon, negotiation window, pickup window, cancel cutoff, no-show points, fares) compiled from published agency rules; offer holds with TTL and stable chained IDs (search_id -> offer_id -> trip_id); idempotency keys and duplicate/overlap detection; linked-trip graph that prompts for orphaned return legs; resumable booking draft; voice-only renderer enforcing 5-option limit; plus an OSS harness that tests Functional Requirements sections 8 and 13 and emits a report shaped like the Local Inspector's documented certification-verdict.json. |
| Simulator or real-data route (data route) | Simulated host (text or browser speech) -> MCP server on Lambda + API Gateway (DynamoDB for sessions, drafts, idempotency keys) -> mock agency scheduler with policy config -> structuredContent + content + ui:// card -> receipt email to test inbox. |
| Authentication | Own OAuth 2.1 authorization server (Cognito) to simulate rider account linking; no payment credentials through form elicitation (spec forbids); stored-value balance simulated; Origin validation. |
| Preview constraints | Gated toolkit, so host is simulated and labelled; Alexa+ payments/checkout API for MCP add-ons not verified; Amazon Pay for classic skills is deprecated; agency is fictional, no real agency branding. |
| Hardware | None required. Classic-skill-on-Echo route optional but risky (see ALX-3 blocker). |
| SDK versions | @modelcontextprotocol/sdk 1.32.0 or server 2.3.0; @modelcontextprotocol/ext-apps 2.0.3; @modelcontextprotocol/inspector 2.9.0 for harness cross-checks; Node 20 LTS. |
| Deployment prerequisites | AWS account (Paid plan if using credits), us-east-1, test email inbox, endpoint live to Nov 20; harness runnable locally without AWS. |
| Firecrawl: research role | Research-time: extracted booking windows, fares, cancel cutoffs and no-show rules from public agency pages to seed policy configs (.firecrawl/idea-alx-paratransit.json). |
| Firecrawl: runtime role | none |
| AWS Builder path | Lambda-hosted sessionful MCP with DynamoDB conditional writes as the idempotency and duplicate-prevention layer; SES receipts (unverified pricing); CloudWatch per-tool-call logs as Amazon's best practice suggests. Modest AWS depth; Kiro Crew or Claude Code on Bedrock also qualifies. |
| Open Source path | Strongest OSS story of the three: 'addon-conformance' harness that replays scripted flows (create, modify, cancel, status, duplicate, interrupted purchase) against any MCP server and emits a verdict JSON; reusable by other Alexa+ entrants; tests included. |
| Fallback | Drop MCP Apps visuals to hydrated data-only cards and keep voice-only path plus harness; if time is short, ship booking+cancel+duplicate only. |
| Falsification test (≤1 day) | Within 1 day: (1) check 5 large agencies' public pages; if most already offer full self-service booking by app and IVR, the gap shrinks to voice-only convenience. (2) Run the voice-only script with 3 testers (ideally one screen-reader user) for 10 book/cancel tasks; if any task mis-books or median completion exceeds 2 minutes, the voice-first premise fails. |
| Risk profile | Technical risk LOW; crowding LOW (no visible paratransit repos); domain-realism risk MEDIUM; platform risk LOW. |
| Estimate (unmeasured) | Solo 88 h; two-person 58 h per person; cloud 3-15 (estimate). Assumptions: Estimates. Includes ~24 h for the OSS harness; no LLM needed at runtime if host uses deterministic rules (forum 45058 allows this), optional Nova Lite under $1; Lambda/API Gateway/DynamoDB near free tier; hosting to Nov 20. |

**Demand evidence** (quality labelled by the researcher):

- FTA ADA compliance reviews confirmed National Council on Disability concerns that long telephone holds are a widespread problem. — [https://dredf.org/ADAtg/tel.shtml](https://dredf.org/ADAtg/tel.shtml) — *Disability-rights legal org topic guide; undated; medium-high*
- RTS: book 1-14 days ahead by app or phone 8am-4pm; +/-1h negotiated times; no-show points; return legs are not auto-cancelled after a first-leg no-show (49 CFR 37.131(b)). — [https://go-rts.com/ada-ridership-policy/](https://go-rts.com/ada-ridership-policy/) — *Official agency policy; high*
- Pace: riders must call the day before to reserve; $3.25 fare via Ventra, tickets or cash; TripCheck can view or cancel. — [https://www.pacebus.com/ada](https://www.pacebus.com/ada) — *Official agency page; high*
- Valley Metro: reservations by phone 1-14 days ahead; cancel at least 2 hours before or it is a no-show; 3+ in 30 days triggers review. — [https://www.valleymetro.org/accessibility/ada-paratransit/how-to-ride](https://www.valleymetro.org/accessibility/ada-paratransit/how-to-ride) — *Official agency page; high*

**Rivals named at generation** (strongest feature; our difference; evidence status):

| Rival | Strongest feature | Our difference | Evidence status |
|---|---|---|---|
| [RTS Plus app (Via-powered)](https://go-rts.com/ada-ridership-policy/) | App booking 1-14 days ahead, annotations (wheelchair, PCA, service animal), cancellation, 10-minutes-out texts. | Voice-first and voice-only booking, linked-leg cancellation prompt, policy disclosure before late cancel; voice support in Via app not verified in reviewed sources. | Agency page read 2026-10-03 |
| [Pace TripCheck](https://www.pacebus.com/ada) | View or cancel scheduled rides online, by phone or app without calling. | Booking itself (Pace page says reservations are by phone) plus voice-only flow. | Agency page read 2026-10-03 |
| [Alexa+ ride booking (Uber)](https://www.aboutamazon.com/news/devices/new-alexa-top-features) | Native voice ride booking via Uber partner. | Paratransit eligibility, advance booking, negotiation windows and no-show rules; paratransit support not verified in reviewed sources. | Vendor page via digest |
| [mcp-voice-simulator (community, for the OSS harness)](https://github.com/AlSayedGamal/mcp-voice-simulator) | Stands in for the gated toolkit: OAuth 2.1 + PKCE linking, tool discovery, pluggable LLM brains. | Harness asserts certification rules (duplicate prevention, isError, stable IDs, confirmation before destructive) rather than simulating conversation. | Repo description only |

**Smallest credible MVP:**

- MCP server: search_trip_options, get_offer, book_trip, modify_trip, cancel_trip, get_trip_status, get_balance with stable IDs and isError contract
- Policy engine with RTS-like and Valley-Metro-like configs and tests
- MCP Apps ui:// carousel, trip card and receipt; voice-only rendering path
- Simulated host with Echo Dot (voice-only) and Echo Show modes; email receipt via test inbox
- OSS conformance harness CLI with 12-15 rule checks and JSON report

**Deliberately excluded stretch:**

- Real agency scheduling integration
- Real payments (stored value simulated)
- Live vehicle tracking
- Eligibility certification workflow

**APIs, events and actions** (status as assessed by the researcher; 'unverified' means not confirmed in reviewed docs, 'gated' means not available to entrants, 'simulated' means not real in the demo):

| Status | API / event / action | Documentation |
|---|---|---|
| verified | Alexa+ add-on Functional Requirements (sections 8 Transaction Flow, 9 Voice-Only, 13 MCP Tool Validation) | https://developer.amazon.com/docs/alexaplus/add-ons/functional-requirements.html |
| verified | MCP Apps tool result _meta.ui.resourceUri + ui:// resources | https://apps.extensions.modelcontextprotocol.io/api/ |
| verified | Alexa+ components: Card, Carousel, confirmations, email confirmations | https://developer.amazon.com/docs/alexaplus/add-ons/mcp-addon-components-and-patterns.html |
| gated | Alexa+ Local Inspector certification-verdict.json format (tool itself gated) | https://developer.amazon.com/docs/alexaplus/add-ons/mcp-toolkit-local-inspector.html |
| unverified | MCP elicitation form mode for missing slots (Alexa+ support unverified) | https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/2025-11-25/client/elicitation |
| simulated | Alexa+ account linking / checkout for fare payment | https://developer.amazon.com/docs/alexaplus/add-ons/mcp-toolkit-account-linking.html |

**Blockers:** Fictional agency and simulated scheduler limit realism; Cannot test with real riders in 20 days; Alexa+ payment/notification APIs unverified, so fare and ETA flows are simulated

**Reasons judges might reject it:** 'Ride booking' is already an Alexa+ category (Uber); Fictional agency reads as a thin wrapper over a mock; Impact claims cannot be shown with real riders; Paratransit is US-specific and unfamiliar to some judges

**Gate result:** overall **pass** (eligibility pass, feasibility pass; native overlap medium, visible crowding low).

- Eligibility: It uses a self-hosted MCP server (2025-11-25 over Streamable HTTP) plus a simulated host with Echo Dot (voice-only) and Echo Show modes. Both are accepted routes (Rules L154; forum 45058, 45263). It implements Amazon's public Functional Requirements but uses no gated tool. The OSS harness only emits JSON shaped like the gated Local Inspector's verdict, which is allowed provided it is never presented as Amazon certification. The agency is fictional, so no real agency is impersonated. Payments are simulated.
- Feasibility: The 88 h solo estimate fits the roughly 117 h capacity (estimate). All dependencies are public: the MCP SDK, ext-apps 2.0.3 and Inspector 2.9.0. No LLM is needed at runtime (forum 45058). DynamoDB conditional writes cover idempotency. The main limits are realism (a fictional agency, no real riders), which are not gates. Real-rider testing within 20 days is not feasible, so no impact claims should be made.
- Conditions: (1) The harness and README say 'unofficial; not Amazon certification'. Remove 'built to Alexa+ certification requirements' from the product name. (2) No real agency names or logos in the UI. Real agency policies are cited only as public sources for the config values. (3) The server accepts 2025-03-26, 2025-06-18 and 2025-11-25 negotiation, because Amazon's lifecycle sample sends 2025-03-26 (mcp.md gaps #1). (4) The endpoint stays live through 20 Nov, or a local-run path is provided.
- Native overlap: Alexa+ already books rides natively through Uber, and 'ride booking' is a partner-gated Category SDK category (mcp.md §2). Forum 45397 warns that existing Alexa+ features score poorly. Paratransit-specific mechanics (advance windows, negotiated times, linked return legs, no-show and cancel policy) were not found natively in reviewed sources, so the pitch must lead with those, not with 'book a ride'.
- Crowding: No visible paratransit repo appears in the 363-repo classification (github.md; a lower bound). The Alexa+ track overall is the most crowded, at about 158 visible repos. The OSS harness sits next to AlSayedGamal/mcp-voice-simulator and everyword's 'verify:live' probe, which checks MCP 2025-11-25 spec rules. That is a different rule set from the Alexa+ Functional Requirements, but the space is adjacent.
- Day-one proof: The deployed endpoint negotiates 2025-11-25 in MCP Inspector and also accepts 2025-03-26. search_trip_options, then get_offer, then book_trip return chained stable IDs. Repeating book_trip with the same idempotency key returns the existing trip_id (DynamoDB conditional write). A ui:// trip card renders in the ext-apps basic host. The voice-only path reads back fare and pickup window and requires an explicit 'yes'.
- Redesign to pass: Not required. A naming and claims hygiene fix only, as listed in the conditions.

**Round-2 judge ratings (1–5; base case: solo, India, VVD on Mac, no Bee hardware, no US Ring device). Weighted = 0.25·Tech + 0.20·Design + 0.20·Impact + 0.20·Originality + 0.15·Feasibility.**

| Judge lens | Tech | Design | Impact | Originality | Feasibility | Weighted | Confidence | One-line verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| devrel | 4 | 3 | 2 | 1 | 4 | 2.80 | high | Well-built but killed: a vendor-substituted job that the Rules would read as a wrapper; keep only the harness. |
| engineer | 3 | 3 | 2 | 1 | 4 | 2.55 | high | Killed: rigorous spec work rebuilding a product category (Spare, Ecolane) that already ships with real scheduler integration; salvage only the conformance harness. |
| product | 3 | 3 | 2 | 1 | 4 | 2.55 | high | Killed: a feasible, tidy rebuild of a product category that transit vendors already ship; salvage only the FR-checks harness as an Open Source piece. |
| **Mean of three** | 3.33 | 3.00 | 2.00 | 1.00 | 4.00 | **2.63** | | Scenario weighted: solo+hw 2.63; duo 2.78; duo+hw 2.78 |

**Why each judge rated it that way (verbatim rationale, one per criterion):**

- *devrel* — Tech: Strongest Alexa+ contract literacy: FR §8/§9/§13 implemented literally (read-back, fare breakdown, duplicate prevention via conditional writes, stable chained IDs, isError), MCP Apps cards, multi-version negotiation (mcp.md §2). Not 5: simulated host, mocked payments. — Design: Voice-only Echo mode as a first-class surface is exactly 'voice-first not voice-only', but the product is a channel adapter onto an agency scheduler, which the Rules call a 'basic MCP wrapper' (JDR-03). — Impact: The job is already delivered by shipping products with real scheduler integration (Spare AI Voice at PSTA/CapMetro, Ecolane IVA), so incremental customer impact is low. — Originality: KILLED by the red team: voice booking/change/cancel without hold time is shipped by Spare, Ecolane and TripMaster; no narrower paratransit framing restores originality. Only the conformance harness is salvageable as an Open Source contribution. — Feasibility: 88 h and low technical risk; feasibility is not the problem.
  - Scenario adjustments: solo+hw tech 4, feasibility 4 (Hardware irrelevant; still killed.); duo tech 4, feasibility 5 (58 h each; still killed.); duo+hw tech 4, feasibility 5 (Still killed.)
- *engineer* — Tech: KILLED by the Oct 3 red team (Spare AI Voice, Ecolane IVA and TripMaster IVA already ship voice paratransit booking inside agency schedulers; PSTA press release 27 Aug 2025); scored for completeness. The engineering is correctness-centred (stable chained IDs, DynamoDB conditional-write idempotency, isError contract, resumable drafts, a voice-only renderer enforcing 5 options) but it is CRUD over a mock scheduler wit… — Design: Voice-only Echo Dot booking with read-back, fare breakdown and explicit consent before cancel is a good device fit and mirrors Amazon's documented display modes, but the host is simulated, the agency fictional, and a rider already has a phone that Spare answers; red team revised design to 3. — Impact: The hold-time problem is real (FTA/DREDF per the Oct 3 digests) but the solution category is already deployed at real agencies with real scheduler integration, so the add-on's incremental case for solving the need collapses; every impact statement is hypothetical with a fictional agency. — Originality: Differentiation fails on every axis the red team tested: voice booking without hold time (Spare, Ecolane, TripMaster), self-service apps (myRTCpara, Via, STA PassApp), native Alexa+ ride booking (Uber; ride booking is a partner-gated Category SDK category), and certification table stakes dressed as features. Red team revised to 1.5; as the only concept judged killed I anchor the bottom of the scale here. — Feasibility: 88 h solo fits about 104 h (estimates) with all-public dependencies, no model and no hardware; the harness is the first cut if short. Feasibility is not the problem.
  - Scenario adjustments: solo+hw tech 3, feasibility 4 (Hardware is irrelevant to a simulated host. No change. Still killed.); duo tech 3, feasibility 5 (At about 58 h each (estimate) with no model or hardware there is ample buffer; headcount does not revive the idea.); duo+hw tech 3, feasibility 5 (Same as duo_no_hw.)
- *product* — Tech: Scored for completeness despite the red-team KILL. MCP server with chained stable IDs, idempotent book_trip, voice-only renderer and a conformance harness (prior mechanism) is a competent transactional MCP implementation; the red team notes sessionful SSE and elicitation fit Lambda poorly and the host understanding is a deterministic grammar (prior_redteam.json ALX-2 feasibility_attack). In a real deployment it shri… — Design: Voice-only booking with read-back, fare breakdown, duplicate catch and linked-leg prompt follows Amazon's Functional Requirements, but those are certification table stakes and the flow is a scripted hotel-style booking chatbot (red team demo_attack). — Impact: Riders' hold-time pain is real, but the headline value is already delivered by shipping vendor agents integrated with real schedulers: Spare AI Voice (CapMetro, PSTA, LA Metro logos; PSTA release 27 Aug 2025), Ecolane IVA, TripMaster IVA (rival matrix; prior_redteam.json). A fictional agency add-on cannot hold agency data, so it cannot realistically serve riders beyond the hackathon. — Originality: KILLED by the red team: voice book/change/cancel without hold is shipped by Spare, Ecolane and TripMaster; self-service booking by myRTCpara, Via-powered apps and others; ride booking is native on Alexa+ via Uber and partner-gated. Residual delta (Echo channel, orphan-leg prompt) does not restore originality (prior_redteam.json differentiation_holds, redesign 'not recommended'). — Feasibility: 88 h solo with low technical risk and no platform dependency (prior estimate; red team says buildable with 20-30 h buffer if OAuth and carousel are trimmed). Feasible does not mean advisable.
  - Scenario adjustments: solo+hw tech 3, feasibility 4 (Hardware set not applicable.); duo tech 3, feasibility 5 (58 h per person, fully simulated, no third-party dependency.); duo+hw tech 3, feasibility 5 (Same as duo without hardware.)

**Round-1 judge ratings (3 Oct, same model; shown for traceability):** devrel 4/4/4/3/5 = 3.95; engineer 4/4/3/3/4 = 3.60; product 4/4/3/3/4 = 3.60

Ecolane IVA and TripMaster IVA offer the same thing on other scheduling platforms.](https://spare.com/products/ai-voice) | 3.5/3/2/1.5/4 | No. (1) Voice booking, changes and cancellations without hold time: already shipped by Spare AI Voice (2024-, PSTA 2025), Ecolane IVA and TripMaster IVA, all integrated with real schedulers and available 24/7. DREDF also notes that some agencies already take bookings through IVR. ---

#### ALX-3: ClaimTrail: multi-week home insurance claim follow-through ledger

**Round:** 1. **Track:** Alexa+ (primary); AWS Builder (mini)

**Pitch:** After a water, fire or storm loss, ClaimTrail keeps the claim's running record for the ~6 weeks to final payment: say expenses and adjuster promises out loud as they happen, snap receipts on a phone, and ask 'what is still owed to us?' to get answers computed from the ledger, not model memory.

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| Primary user | US homeowners and renters displaced or disrupted by a covered property loss; a helper relative as secondary user. |
| Frequency | Episodic per household but multi-week per claim: J.D. Power 2026 average repair 29.6 days and 40.7 days to final payment. Household claim incidence not verified in reviewed sources. |
| Severity | High financial stakes: 34% say the policy did not fully meet expectations; common issues include unexplained estimates and out-of-pocket costs (J.D. Power 2026). Public adjusters market ALE trackers to 'get back every dollar'. |
| Current workaround | Insurer portal (e.g., USAA My Claims Center), public-adjuster ALE spreadsheets, home-inventory apps, notes and a shoebox of receipts. |
| Amazon integration and its indispensable role | Echo in the (temporary) home captures entries hands-free at the moment they happen; Echo Show shows a claim board; Alexa answers 'what is due next' across weeks. State lives server-side across sessions (rubric's 'context-aware add-on'). Real Echo via classic skill if feasible, else simulated host. |
| Removal test (honest) | Without Alexa+ this is a claim-ledger web app and largely survives. Alexa adds capture-at-the-moment and ambient status, a moderate dependence. Extra risk: Alexa+ native memory and document uploads could absorb simple 'remember I paid X' (existing-feature risk); the defensible part is reconciliation and commitments, which are not Alexa-specific. |
| Wow moment under 45 s | On an Echo Show (classic skill, dev mode) or simulator: 'Tell ClaimTrail the hotel charged 189 tonight and the adjuster said the estimate comes Friday.' Board updates: ALE 1,323 over 7 nights; new commitment 'Adjuster: estimate by Fri' with due tag. Phone uploads the receipt; Textract reads 189.00 and the voice entry flips to reconciled. 'What is still owed to us?' -> 'About 2,140: 1,323 living expenses submitted not paid, 817 recoverable depreciation pending the repair invoice', with source chips. |
| Original mechanism | Event-sourced claim ledger with evidence pointers; reconciliation engine (claimed vs approved vs paid per category, recoverable-depreciation holdback tracking); commitment tracker (who promised what by when, overdue detection); voice-entry to receipt matching via Textract AnalyzeExpense; deadlines only from dates the user enters from their own policy (no insurance advice); exportable claim packet. |
| Simulator or real-data route (data route) | Echo utterance -> classic skill Lambda (ASK SDK) -> MCP client call -> MCP server -> DynamoDB event store; phone upload -> S3 -> Textract AnalyzeExpense -> matcher -> ledger; APL board and Reminders back to Echo; simulated host reads the same MCP server. |
| Authentication | Skill account linking to own Cognito user pool (simulated household account); MCP endpoint JWT; S3 presigned uploads; receipts are personal financial data, so encryption at rest and delete/export controls. |
| Preview constraints | Native Alexa+ add-on deployment is gated; classic skill route is forum-approved (45263) but an Amazon Forum thread (Jan 2026) reports a developer skill stopped working on an Echo Dot after Alexa+ Early Access, and India Early Access began 16 Sep 2026, so the demo device may need to leave Early Access. |
| Hardware | Optional Echo Show or Echo Dot (India availability of specific models not verified); phone for receipt photos; Mac. |
| SDK versions | ask-sdk-core for Node (version not verified); @modelcontextprotocol/sdk 1.32.0 client in skill Lambda; Node 20 Lambda runtime; AWS SDK v3 Textract client. |
| Deployment prerequisites | Amazon Developer account for the skill (dev-mode testing on own account), AWS account in us-east-1, Echo registered to same account, verify day 1 that a dev skill runs on the device. |
| Firecrawl: research role | Research-time: J.D. Power study, rival claim and inventory tools, classic-skill-on-Alexa+ compatibility reports (.firecrawl/idea-alx-property-claims.json, idea-alx-claim-rivals.json, idea-alx-classic-skills-alexaplus.json). |
| Firecrawl: runtime role | none |
| AWS Builder path | Textract AnalyzeExpense does real work (receipt totals, vendor, date) that feeds reconciliation; Lambda for skill and MCP; DynamoDB event store; S3 for evidence; optional Nova Lite only for splitting a compound utterance into entries. |
| Open Source path | MIT repo with the reconciliation/commitment engine as a standalone library and synthetic claim scenarios as test fixtures; optionally contribute the shared conformance harness from ALX-2. |
| Fallback | Drop the real-Echo path and demo the same MCP server in a simulated Echo Show host; keep Textract matching and the ledger. |
| Falsification test (≤1 day) | Within 1 day: interview 5 people who filed a property claim in the last 2 years; if 1 or fewer report losing track of expenses, payments or adjuster promises, or most say the insurer portal sufficed, the premise fails. Also run 30 scripted voice entries; if amount or category errors exceed 10 percent after read-back, voice capture is not viable. |
| Risk profile | Domain-sensitivity risk HIGH; platform risk MEDIUM-HIGH on real Echo (LOW on fallback); crowding MEDIUM; technical risk MEDIUM. |
| Estimate (unmeasured) | Solo 92 h; two-person 62 h per person; cloud 8-30 (estimate). Assumptions: Estimates. Includes ~16 h for the classic skill and APL; Textract pricing not verified in reviewed sources, assumed under $10 for fewer than 200 pages; Lambda/DynamoDB/S3 near free tier; Nova Lite under $1; hosting to Nov 20. |

**Demand evidence** (quality labelled by the researcher):

- J.D. Power 2026 Property Claims Study (n=5,093, released 17 Mar 2026): repair 29.6 days, final payment 40.7 days; 34% say policy did not fully meet expectations. — [https://www.jdpower.com/business/press-releases/2026-us-property-claims-satisfaction-study/](https://www.jdpower.com/business/press-releases/2026-us-property-claims-satisfaction-study/) — *Industry research firm; high*
- Claims Journal coverage confirms fieldwork Dec 2024-Dec 2025 and digital tool use (38% FNOL, 49% photos, 45% updates). — [https://www.claimsjournal.com/news/national/2026/03/18/336327.htm](https://www.claimsjournal.com/news/national/2026/03/18/336327.htm) — *Trade press; medium-high*
- Public adjuster offers a free ALE tracker; says decisions in the first six months after a fire largely determine settlement. — [https://www.millerpublicadjusters.com/ale-worksheet](https://www.millerpublicadjusters.com/ale-worksheet) — *Vendor with interest; low-medium*
- Bevel was built after the 2025 LA fires to turn photos/videos into itemized loss inventories. — [https://www.nerdwallet.com/insurance/homeowners/learn/home-inventory-app-template](https://www.nerdwallet.com/insurance/homeowners/learn/home-inventory-app-template) — *Editorial review; medium*

**Rivals named at generation** (strongest feature; our difference; evidence status):

| Rival | Strongest feature | Our difference | Evidence status |
|---|---|---|---|
| [USAA My Claims Center](https://mobile.usaa.com/support/insurance/claims/homeowners/faq/) | Insurer-side claim status, add details, message adjuster, photo submission. | Policyholder-owned ledger across insurer, contractors and expenses with reconciliation; insurer-agnostic. | FAQ page read 2026-10-03 |
| [Miller Public Adjusters ALE Tracker](https://www.millerpublicadjusters.com/ale-worksheet) | Free downloadable ALE worksheet from claim professionals. | Live capture, receipt matching and paid-vs-claimed reconciliation; spreadsheet is manual. | Landing page read |
| [Nest Egg / NAIC Home Inventory / Under My Roof](https://sagesure.com/insurance-insights/home-inventory-apps-to-document-your-possessions/) | Pre-loss inventories with photos, receipts, barcode scan; Under My Roof has insurance-specific reports. | Post-loss follow-through (expenses, promises, payments), not pre-loss inventory. | Third-party article (Oct 2025) |
| [homebrain (visible entrant)](https://github.com/ramkumar122/homebrain) | Durable MCP memory for home repairs, warranties and service cases. | Insurance-claim money reconciliation and adjuster commitments; overlap on 'durable home case memory'. | Repo description only |

**Smallest credible MVP:**

- MCP server: log_expense, log_commitment, attach_receipt, get_claim_status, reconcile_payments, export_packet
- Event-sourced ledger in DynamoDB with tests on 3 reconstructed claim scenarios
- Phone web upload + Textract AnalyzeExpense matching
- Classic custom skill (AMAZON.SearchQuery capture, APL board) calling the MCP server; simulated host as fallback
- Packet export (PDF/CSV) with evidence links

**Deliberately excluded stretch:**

- Insurer integrations or FNOL filing
- Coverage interpretation or claim advice
- Contractor marketplace
- Home inventory capture

**APIs, events and actions** (status as assessed by the researcher; 'unverified' means not confirmed in reviewed docs, 'gated' means not available to entrants, 'simulated' means not real in the demo):

| Status | API / event / action | Documentation |
|---|---|---|
| unverified | ASK custom skill with AMAZON.SearchQuery slot | https://developer.amazon.com/en-US/docs/alexa/custom-skills/slot-type-reference.html#searchquery |
| unverified | Alexa Presentation Language (APL) for Echo Show board | https://developer.amazon.com/en-US/docs/alexa/alexa-presentation-language/understand-apl.html |
| unverified | Alexa Reminders API for user-entered deadlines | https://developer.amazon.com/en-US/docs/alexa/smapi/alexa-reminders-overview.html |
| unverified | Amazon Textract AnalyzeExpense | https://docs.aws.amazon.com/textract/latest/dg/analyzing-document-expense.html |
| verified | MCP 2025-11-25 Streamable HTTP server called by skill backend | https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/2025-11-25/basic/transports |
| gated | Alexa+ add-on (native, no invocation name) | https://developer.amazon.com/docs/alexaplus/add-ons/mcp-toolkit-overview.html |

**Blockers:** Dev skill may not run on an Alexa+ Early Access Echo (forum anecdote); Insurance domain invites 'advice' expectations and liability concerns; Realistic claim data must be synthetic or reconstructed

**Reasons judges might reject it:** Overlaps Alexa+ native memory and document uploads; Episodic use; impact per household is rare; Insurance/financial sensitivity and no real insurer integration; Home-repair/case-memory entrants already visible

**Gate result:** overall **conditional** (eligibility pass, feasibility conditional; native overlap medium, visible crowding low).

- Eligibility: Two forum-accepted routes are eligible: a classic Alexa Skill on an Echo that invokes your MCP (forum 45263), or a simulated host (forum 45058). The native Alexa+ add-on is correctly marked gated and excluded. There is no claim of live Alexa+ integration. Insurance data is synthetic or reconstructed, so no real insurer data or branding is used.
- Feasibility: The MVP includes a classic custom skill on a real Echo. The base-case user has no Echo, and an Amazon Forum thread (Jan 2026) reports personal and developer skills failing under Alexa+ Early Access. Alexa+ Early Access began in India on 16 Sep 2026 (mcp.md §11), so a device in India may be enrolled. Textract AnalyzeExpense and the ASK pieces are marked unverified in the concept. The 92 h solo estimate fits (estimate). The simulated-host fallback removes the hardware dependency.
- Conditions: (1) The default demo runs on the simulated Echo Show host. Show a real Echo only if the user has one registered to the same developer account and a dev-mode skill is confirmed working on it outside Alexa+ Early Access. (2) Claims and receipts are synthetic or reconstructed and labelled. No coverage interpretation or insurance advice. (3) Receipts are treated as personal financial data: encrypted at rest, with delete and export controls. (4) The MCP endpoint stays live through 20 Nov, or a local-run path is provided. Bedrock is optional, used only for splitting compound utterances.
- Native overlap: Alexa+ natively remembers personal facts, accepts document uploads, manages email and calendar and sets reminders (aboutamazon, updated 24 Jul 2026). That could absorb simple 'remember I paid 189'. Reconciliation of claimed, approved and paid amounts, depreciation holdback, and adjuster commitment tracking were not found natively. Insurer portals (USAA) are the incumbent substitute.
- Crowding: No visible home-insurance-claim entrant was found. Adjacent visible repos are ramkumar122/homebrain (durable home case memory), Marc-Dvci/Circa (home-repair transaction guardian), K1Andayesh/fixproof (repair handover evidence) and BxRKings/claimguard-voice (shipment claims). The Alexa+ track overall is crowded, at about 158 visible repos.
- Day-one proof: The deployed MCP server passes Inspector. Textract AnalyzeExpense extracts vendor, total and date correctly on at least 9 of 10 of the team's own receipts. Ledger reconciliation unit tests pass on one reconstructed claim. Only if the Echo path is pursued: a dev-mode custom skill on the user's own Echo invokes the MCP-backed Lambda end to end.
- Redesign to pass: To make it unconditional, drop the classic-skill/Echo path from the MVP and demo only on the simulated Echo Show host. Lead with reconciliation ('what is still owed and why', with source chips) rather than voice capture, so it does not read as Alexa+ memory.

**Round-2 judge ratings (1–5; base case: solo, India, VVD on Mac, no Bee hardware, no US Ring device). Weighted = 0.25·Tech + 0.20·Design + 0.20·Impact + 0.20·Originality + 0.15·Feasibility.**

| Judge lens | Tech | Design | Impact | Originality | Feasibility | Weighted | Confidence | One-line verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| devrel | 3 | 3 | 3 | 3 | 4 | 3.15 | medium | Feasible and evidence-backed, but episodic use and native-memory overlap keep it mid-table. |
| engineer | 3 | 3 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 2.95 | medium | Feasible and uncrowded but conventional: voice adds little, native Alexa+ memory overlaps it, and the pain is thinly evidenced. |
| product | 3 | 3 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 2.95 | medium | Useful but rarely-needed ledger whose Alexa+ dependence is light; impact per household high, reach and frequency low. |
| **Mean of three** | 3.00 | 3.00 | 2.33 | 3.00 | 4.00 | **3.02** | | Scenario weighted: solo+hw 3.02; duo 3.15; duo+hw 3.15 |

**Why each judge rated it that way (verbatim rationale, one per criterion):**

- *devrel* — Tech: Event-sourced ledger with reconciliation and MCP 2025-11-25 gives real 'state across sessions'; Textract AnalyzeExpense unverified and the Alexa layer is a simulated host over a domain ledger. — Design: Capture-at-the-moment voice plus phone receipts plus an Echo Show claim board is sensible, but native Alexa+ memory and document uploads absorb the simple half (forum 45397 risk, JDR-04), and it must avoid looking like insurance advice. — Impact: High-quality evidence (J.D. Power 2026 n=5,093: 40.7 days to payment) for a high-stakes but episodic household event; demo data must be synthetic. — Originality: No visible insurance-claim entrant; reconciliation and adjuster-commitment tracking are not native; adjacent durable home-case memory entrants (homebrain, Circa) and a new 6-repo appliance/repair cluster exist. — Feasibility: 92 h minus the ~16 h classic-skill path the gate drops leaves comfortable slack.
  - Scenario adjustments: solo+hw tech 3, feasibility 4 (An Echo adds a real surface only if a dev skill works outside Alexa+ Early Access (unverified).); duo tech 4, feasibility 5 (62 h each; AnalyzeExpense matching fully lands.); duo+hw tech 4, feasibility 5 (As duo.)
- *engineer* — Tech: An MCP server over an event-sourced claim ledger with a reconciliation engine (claimed vs approved vs paid, depreciation holdback) and Textract AnalyzeExpense receipt matching is real domain logic, but AnalyzeExpense is unverified in reviewed sources and the base case drops the classic-skill/APL path (dev skills reportedly break under Alexa+ Early Access; India Early Access began 16 Sep 2026 per mcp.md section 11), … — Design: Voice capture, an Echo Show claim board and phone receipts are plausible, but the removal test admits the product survives as a web app and native Alexa+ memory and document upload can absorb 'remember I paid X' (aboutamazon, updated 24 Jul 2026). — Impact: J.D. Power 2026 context (40.7 days to final payment) is good, but the evidence that policyholders lose track of owed amounts comes from a public adjuster with an interest; use is episodic per household and the product must avoid insurance advice. — Originality: No visible insurance-claim entrant; reconciliation with holdback tracking is specific. Adjacent home-case entrants (homebrain, Circa, fixproof, the appliance-repair cluster of about 6 on Oct 4) and native memory reduce novelty. — Feasibility: 92 h includes about 16 h for the classic skill that the gate condition drops, so about 76 h effective against about 104 h (estimates); simulated host, Lambda/DynamoDB near free tier, Bedrock optional. The remaining unknown is AnalyzeExpense accuracy.
  - Scenario adjustments: solo+hw tech 3, feasibility 4 (No Echo in the hardware set, so the simulated host remains. No change.); duo tech 3, feasibility 4 (Hours comfortable; the mechanism ceiling and unverified AnalyzeExpense do not move with headcount.); duo+hw tech 3, feasibility 4 (Same as duo_no_hw.)
- *product* — Tech: MCP server with an event-sourced ledger, reconciliation engine, Textract AnalyzeExpense receipt matching and a simulated Echo Show host (prior mechanism); the classic-skill-on-Echo path is risky under Alexa+ Early Access and the gate recommends dropping it. Solid but conventional; Alexa layer simulated. — Design: Voice capture at the moment plus a board and source chips is coherent, and leading with reconciliation (gate redesign_to_pass) avoids reading as Alexa+ memory. Episodic, phone-plus-voice workflow; nothing device-specific beyond ambient capture. — Impact: Severity is high for a displaced household during a 6-week claim and workarounds exist (USAA My Claims Center, Miller ALE Tracker worksheet, rival matrix), which shows the need. But frequency per household is rare, the population at any moment is small, it is US-insurer specific, and no reviewed source shows claimants wanting a voice tool (prior risks 'episodic use'). — Originality: Policyholder-owned reconciliation of claimed vs approved vs paid with commitment tracking is not verified in USAA, ALE trackers or inventory apps (rival matrix). Adjacent visible entrants: ramkumar122/homebrain and the appliance-repair/handover cluster of about 6 (refresh). Alexa+ native memory and document uploads absorb the simple capture half. — Feasibility: 92 h solo (prior estimate) with the Echo path dropped; Textract AnalyzeExpense accuracy check on day 1; synthetic claims. Low platform risk.
  - Scenario adjustments: solo+hw tech 3, feasibility 4 (An Echo is not in the hardware set; the simulated host remains the default.); duo tech 3, feasibility 4 (62 h per person; buffer for the APL/Echo path if a dev-mode skill works.); duo+hw tech 3, feasibility 4 (Same as duo without hardware.)

**Round-1 judge ratings (3 Oct, same model; shown for traceability):** devrel 3/3/3/3/4 = 3.15; engineer 3/3/2/3/4 = 2.95; product 3/3/3/3/4 = 3.15

### Track: Bee

#### BEE-1: Said & Done: a two-key promise board for households (Bee + Fire TV)

**Round:** 1. **Track:** Bee (primary). The Fire TV (Vega) app is a secondary surface. Multi-track judging is allowed, but a project can win only one track prize.

**Pitch:** Promises spoken at home are captured by Bee and become proposals that carry the verbatim quote. The other person accepts or declines on the living-room Fire TV. Any later change must be accepted again, and an item closes only when the counterparty confirms it is done. This is accepted ownership, not another to-do list.

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| Primary user | Two-adult households where one adult wears Bee and the other does not, for example working parents splitting school, medical and home logistics. The non-wearer acts through the shared Fire TV with no app to install. Assumption: the household has a Fire TV; the demo may use the Vega Virtual Device instead. |
| Frequency | Inference, not measured: daily. Household promises ('I'll call the plumber', 'can you renew the car insurance?') are made in passing on most days. The falsification test counts real promises per evening. |
| Severity | Medium (inference). Missed promises cause friction, late fees and missed appointments, not safety harm. Related evidence: parents report overcommunication and multi-platform burden (Romper, citing a 2026 systematic review), and Bee reviewers report to-do overload and poor speaker attribution (Forbes Vetted). |
| Current workaround | Bee's own suggested to-dos and commitment recall, which are single-user. Otherwise WhatsApp or text threads, hand-typed shared lists (Cozi, Hearth, Skylight), or memory. No reviewed source describes a spoken promise becoming an item the other person must explicitly accept, revise and close. |
| Amazon integration and its indispensable role | Bee is the only capture source and also the write-back target: wearer-owned accepted items become Bee todos with alarms, and completions are read from the changefeed. The Fire TV Vega app is the shared household screen where the non-wearer accepts, declines, approves revisions and confirms completion with the D-pad. AWS (API Gateway, Lambda, DynamoDB, Bedrock) is the reachable backend between the laptop-bound Bee CLI and the TV. |
| Removal test (honest) | Without Bee, the product becomes a manually typed shared board, which is Cozi, Hearth and Caring Village territory. The core is lost: automatic capture of spoken promises with verbatim evidence, and revision linking. So Bee is indispensable. Without Fire TV, the board still works as a phone web page. Fire TV is a surface choice: it reaches the non-wearer without an install and matches the organizers' cross-device example, but it is not indispensable. Weakness: Bee already extracts the wearer's commitments natively. Our value rests entirely on the second key (acceptance), the revision chain and confirmed close-out. Their absence in rivals is unverified, not proven. |
| Wow moment under 45 s | On camera (consenting adults), the partner asks: 'Can you book Riya's dentist appointment before Friday? Her tooth is still bothering her.' The wearer replies at length, ending with 'I'll call them tomorrow morning' (well over 50 words). A cut with an on-screen clock shows the real processing delay. The Fire TV board (VVD or a Fire TV Stick) shows a new Proposed card: 'Arjun to Meera: book dentist before Fri', with the quote and time. The partner presses Select > Accept. The card moves to Accepted, and on the wearer's iPhone the Bee app shows a new todo with a Thursday 9:00 alarm. Second beat: the wearer later says 'they only had Monday', and the card turns amber: 'Changed, needs Meera's OK'. |
| Original mechanism | A two-key commitment ledger. Each spoken promise becomes a record with an evidence pointer: Bee conversation_uuid, utterance text, speaker label and conversation start_time. Every state transition needs a different party for each key. The speaker proposes and the counterparty accepts; later, the owner claims done and the counterparty confirms. DynamoDB conditional writes enforce this, so a double accept or a self-accept is impossible. A later utterance is linked as a revision by matching counterpart plus semantic similarity, and it reopens the acceptance gate. Privacy is enforced by construction: speakers not mapped to a household member (guests) are dropped before storage, quotes on unaccepted items expire via TTL after 72 h, and a decline deletes the quote. Only wearer-owned accepted items are mirrored to Bee todos with alarm_at, and their completion is read back through `bee changed`… |
| Simulator or real-data route (data route) | Bee Pioneer or Watch > Bee cloud processing > Bee CLI on the Mac. `bee stream --json` gives liveness; `bee changed --cursor` every 60 s gives completeness. For each processed conversation the daemon runs `bee conversations transcript <id> --json`, then a local filter drops unmapped speakers. Only the filtered utterance window goes to Bedrock (Nova Lite) for extraction. The daemon POSTs derived records (text, a quote of 25 words or fewer, ids, times, never the full transcript) to API Gateway, then Lambda, then DynamoDB (conditional writes, TTL).
