**Blockers:** Children's data and COPPA perception: a real Alexa deployment would require Amazon Kids and kids-skill certification with verifiable parental consent; the demo can only simulate and declare this; Two-sided adoption: host families must onboard once; without it the product degrades to single-household check-ins; No enforcement: a child can leave without asking; the product is a trust tool, which some judges will call safety theatre; Real SMS from AWS needs SNS sandbox exit and US number registration; the MVP uses a mock inbox, which weakens 'looks functional' unless staged well; Sessionful Streamable HTTP on Lambda+API Gateway is awkward if elicitation is used; OutPass avoids elicitation (parent decisions are their own tool calls), which keeps Lambda viable

**Reasons judges might reject it:** Reads as 'reminders plus a message to Mom', which Alexa+ already does (latent capability / basic alert); Children's data and COPPA sensitivity; judges from Amazon know real kid experiences need Amazon Kids certification; Trust-based design with no location may be judged as not actually protecting the child; Two-sided host-family adoption looks hard to bootstrap; the demo depends on actors playing three roles; US-centric framing (Wait Until 8th); impact evidence is movement-level, not incident data; Overlap with the visible 'helper acceptance / handoff' entrants (KindHandoff, homerelay) in the general acceptance pattern

**Gate result:** overall **pass** (eligibility pass, feasibility pass; native overlap medium, visible crowding medium).

- Eligibility: Same accepted route as ALX-1..4: a self-hosted MCP server (2025-11-25, Streamable HTTP) plus a labelled simulated Alexa+-style host with three surfaces (Rules §4; forum 45058, 45403). Voice ID child attribution, Alexa notifications and Amazon Kids parental consent are correctly listed as simulated or unavailable and not claimed. No location data, no real SMS (SNS sandbox excluded), synthetic families, mock inbox labelled. Real Alexa children's experiences would require Amazon Kids certification and COPPA-compliant consent; because the Alexa layer is simulated and declared, no rule is broken, but the README must say the real deployment path needs that review. Actors' voices in the video need…
- Feasibility: The 92 h solo estimate fits the roughly 104 h capacity (estimate), with about 12 h of buffer. The pass state machine, deterministic rule engine (NOAA sunset formula is public), signed one-time links and DynamoDB conditional writes are standard; Step Functions Wait states with task-token cancellation were verified for ALX-1 on Oct 3. The design avoids server-initiated elicitation (parent decisions are their own tool calls), which keeps Lambda plus API Gateway viable. No LLM at runtime by default. The largest build is the three-surface simulated host (20 h). The human falsification test (three families with 8-13-year-olds) is a recruiting sensitivity, not a build blocker; the demo uses actors…
- Conditions: (1) The host is labelled a simulation; the README states that a real children's Alexa experience would need Amazon Kids certification and verifiable parental consent, which the demo does not claim. (2) No GPS or location data anywhere, including inferred location; the privacy statement in the README says so. (3) Server accepts 2025-03-26, 2025-06-18 and 2025-11-25 negotiation; hosted endpoint live through 20 Nov or a local-run path is documented. (4) Child voices in the video are adult voice actors or minors with written guardian consent; all participants consent to publication. (5) Real SMS and email stay excluded; the mock inbox is labelled on screen so the host-family confirmation does not look falsely live. (6) Step Functions and DynamoDB in us-east-1 or us-west-2; Bedrock Nova Lite only after a day-1 quota check, with the deterministic parser as default.
- Native overlap: Alexa+ natively offers per-family-member routines with voice recognition, Visual-ID reminders, calling and messaging on Echo devices and Kids+ guardrails (aboutamazon, updated 24 Jul 2026), so 'Alexa, tell Mom I'm going to Rohan's' is a latent message and forum 45397 warns that existing-feature ideas score poorly. Apple Check In is the closest consumer analogue but 'requires iOS 17 or later for both the sender and recipient' (Apple support page, read 2026-10-04), so it excludes a phone-free chi…
- Crowding: The Alexa+ track is the most crowded (about 158+ visible repos; about 53 per prize). The theme is uncrowded: GitHub searches on 2026-10-04 returned 'curfew alexa created:>2026-08-31' = 0 and 'kids permission "check-in" alexa' = 0, and no kids-permission or phone-free repo appears in the saved sets. Adjacent patterns are visible: daily check-in with caregiver escalation for someone living alone (MccForge/hearth, skiitk2510/carecompanion), the household handoff/acceptance cluster (about 13: KindH…
- Day-one proof: The deployed endpoint negotiates 2025-11-25 in Inspector 2.9.0 and accepts a 2025-03-26 initialize; check_out starts a Step Functions execution whose first Wait state would fire a reminder, and check_in sends task success so no reminder fires (verified in the execution history); a duplicate check_in with the same client event ID is a no-op (DynamoDB conditional write); the sunset-curfew function matches the NOAA solar calculator within 2 minutes for three cities and dates; a ui:// pass card with countdown renders in the ext-apps basic-host; an HMAC-signed one-time host link verifies once and rejects reuse.
- Redesign to pass: Not required. To blunt the 'reminder plus message' reading, show the host-family confirmation and the ladder cancelling atomically on check-in in the first 30 s, and put the no-location principle on screen. If two-household adoption worries judges, demo single-household mode first and layer host confirmation as the second beat.

**Round-2 judge ratings (1–5; base case: solo, India, VVD on Mac, no Bee hardware, no US Ring device). Weighted = 0.25·Tech + 0.20·Design + 0.20·Impact + 0.20·Originality + 0.15·Feasibility.**

| Judge lens | Tech | Design | Impact | Originality | Feasibility | Weighted | Confidence | One-line verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| devrel | 3 | 3 | 3 | 3 | 4 | 3.15 | medium | Clean, feasible and well-fitted to the Echo, but COPPA adjacency and 'reminder plus message' perception cap it. |
| engineer | 4 | 4 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 3.40 | high | The cleanest Alexa+ architecture and the strongest Echo-as-only-terminal fit, let down by movement-level impact evidence and two-household adoption. |
| product | 3 | 4 | 3 | 4 | 4 | 3.55 | medium | Original, privacy-first and the most completable Alexa+ concept; impact rests on movement-level evidence and two-household adoption, and judges may read it as reminders plus messaging unless the ladder and host handshake lead. |
| **Mean of three** | 3.33 | 3.67 | 2.67 | 3.33 | 4.00 | **3.37** | | Scenario weighted: solo+hw 3.37; duo 3.55; duo+hw 3.55 |

**Why each judge rated it that way (verbatim rationale, one per criterion):**

- *devrel* — Tech: Real MCP server with idempotent transitions, a Step Functions ladder with task-token cancellation (verified Oct 3), signed one-time links and ui:// cards; deliberately avoids elicitation so Lambda hosting is clean. Capped: simulated host, mock inbox for host-family confirmation, no model. — Design: The phone-free child as acting user makes the Echo the only possible terminal (strong fit), but real children's experiences need Amazon Kids certification and consent, the mock inbox weakens 'looks functional' (JDR-06), and 'tell Mom I'm going to Rohan's' is a native message. — Impact: Daily for a sizeable organised segment (Wait Until 8th 155,000 pledges, JDR-17) and Apple Check In shows the pattern is wanted but is iPhone-to-iPhone only (JDR-18); evidence is movement-level, not incident data, and two-sided adoption is hard. — Originality: Child-initiated pass with cross-household confirmation and a no-GPS ladder was not found natively or in the gate's searches, but the acceptance/handoff form overlaps a 13-repo cluster and elder check-in ladders in form. — Feasibility: 92 h with ~12 h buffer, low architectural risk, actors for the demo.
  - Scenario adjustments: solo+hw tech 3, feasibility 4 (Hardware irrelevant.); duo tech 4, feasibility 5 (62 h each; three-surface host gets polish.); duo+hw tech 4, feasibility 5 (As duo.)
- *engineer* — Tech: Same MCP stack as ALX-4 (2025-11-25 Streamable HTTP with multi-version negotiation, ToolAnnotations, MCP Apps ui:// via ext-apps 2.0.3) but with the architecture resolved: parent decisions are their own tool calls, so no server-initiated elicitation and Lambda plus API Gateway stays viable; the escalation ladder is Step Functions Wait states with task-token cancellation on check_in (the pattern verified for ALX-1 on… — Design: Strongest Alexa+ device fit of the five: a phone-free child has no other terminal, so the Echo is the only possible interface rather than a trigger; three simulated surfaces (kitchen Echo Show, parent Alexa app, host-family link) with a pass card, countdown, conditions and a ladder that cancels on check-in; no location data by construction. Deductions: the mock inbox and simulated Voice ID weaken 'looks functional',… — Impact: Specific audience and a real movement (Wait Until 8th: 155,000+ pledges, average first smartphone at ten; saved alx2-demand-waituntil8th.md, advocacy counter), but evidence is movement-level with no incident data, the product has no enforcement (a trust tool), it needs two households to onboard, and Apple Check In (iOS 17 both ends) and kid watches are the substitutes families actually buy. — Originality: Uncrowded theme (gate: 'curfew alexa' = 0, 'kids permission check-in alexa' = 0 on Oct 4) and a distinct mechanism (child-initiated pass, cross-household confirmation, no-GPS watchdog ladder, trust ledger). Offsets: it can read as 'reminder plus a message to Mom', which Alexa+ does natively, and the acceptance pattern overlaps the household handoff cluster (about 13 repos: KindHandoff, homerelay). — Feasibility: 92 h solo against about 104 h (estimates) with about 12 h of buffer, no hardware, no LLM by default, Lambda-compatible design and verified Step Functions patterns; the three-surface host (20 h) is the largest item and single-household mode is a clean fallback. The human falsification test is a recruiting sensitivity only.
  - Scenario adjustments: solo+hw tech 4, feasibility 4 (Hardware is irrelevant to a simulated Alexa+ host. No change.); duo tech 4, feasibility 4 (At about 62 h each (estimate) backend/AWS and host/UI/video split cleanly; the ceiling stays at 4 because the Alexa layer is simulated by rule.); duo+hw tech 4, feasibility 4 (Same as duo_no_hw.)
- *product* — Tech: MCP server with a pass state machine, DynamoDB conditional writes, Step Functions Wait-state escalation ladder with task-token cancellation, HMAC signed one-time host links and MCP Apps pass card (concept mvp_scope; gate day_one_proof); avoids elicitation so Lambda hosting is clean. Comparable MCP depth to ALX-1; the three-surface simulated host is UI work rather than required-tech depth; Voice ID child attribution,… — Design: The child's only terminal is the kitchen Echo, so voice-first-not-voice-only is intrinsic; pass card with countdown and conditions, parent approval with conditions on the Alexa app surface, host confirmation by one-tap link, ladder cancelled atomically on check-in, no location anywhere (concept mechanism; gate redesign_to_pass). Not 5: three roles played by actors, a mock inbox stands in for SMS, and a real children… — Impact: Wait Until 8th shows an organised segment: more than 155,000 parents pledged, average first smartphone at ten, pledgers pointed to call-and-text-only devices (alx2-demand-waituntil8th.md). Apple Check In requires iOS 17 on both ends (alx2-rival-apple-checkin.json) and Life360 presupposes a tracked phone or Tile, so phone-free children have no equivalent. But evidence is movement-level (no incident data), the product… — Originality: No kids-permission, curfew or phone-free entrant in visible repos (gate: 0 results for two GitHub queries). Amazon Kids offers contact approval for calls and parental consent flows but no child check-in or permission-pass feature surfaced in my live search (pj2-amazon-kids-alexa.json; absence not proof). Alexa+ can relay 'tell Mom I'm going to Rohan's' as a message (rival matrix row 1), but a structured pass with co… — Feasibility: 92 h solo leaves about 12 h buffer (gate; estimate); no new toolchain, no LLM at runtime, no elicitation, Step Functions Wait states verified in round 1; the tabletop with three families is optional validation. Hosting through 20 Nov required only if the endpoint is referenced.
  - Scenario adjustments: solo+hw tech 3, feasibility 4 (Mac-only; the hardware set does not apply.); duo tech 3, feasibility 5 (62 h per person, fully simulated, no third-party or hardware dependency: the most completable concept as a duo.); duo+hw tech 3, feasibility 5 (Same as duo without hardware.)

### Track: Bee

#### BEE-4: Hearsay: who actually said that? Evidence-checked review of the facts Bee believes about you

**Round:** 2. **Track:** Bee (primary). Priority use cases: personal productivity and developer experience (hygiene of the memory layer that agents consume). Mini challenges: AWS Builder (Bedrock judge models; built with Claude Code on Bedrock) and Open Source (bee-evidence library plus a LICENSE PR to bee-skill). No Fire TV, Echo or Ring surface.

**Pitch:** Bee turns conversations into 'facts' about the wearer that must be confirmed one by one, and reviewers report facts lifted from podcasts and TV, other people's statements attributed to the wearer, and facts that contradict each other from day to day. Hearsay audits every pending fact against the verbatim speaker-labelled utterances Bee recorded: who said it (the wearer, another speaker about the wearer, another speaker about themselves, or a one-voice monologue that looks like media), when it was spoken versus when Bee created the fact, and whether it contradicts or stales out a confirmed fact. One keyboard-driven review screen batch-confirms, corrects or deletes through Bee's own facts write API, and exports a trusted, evidence-linked profile that coding and personal agents load instead of a raw facts dump.

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| Primary user | Bee wearers who use the facts layer downstream: people who connect Bee to Claude Code, Codex or Kiro through the Bee Agent Skill or MCP (the Skill's 'Deep Learning About the Owner' workflow and bee_list_facts feed agents from facts), and anyone whose pending-facts queue has outgrown one-by-one tapping in the app. Assumption (unmeasured): a minority of Bee users, sized from reviews. Demo needs only the wearer plus one consenting friend. |
| Frequency | Per the Forbes hands-on review (byline 24 Jul 2026, updated 16 Sep 2026) every fact and to-do needs approval and the queue becomes unmanageable after a missed day, and many facts were pulled from TV shows, podcasts and other people, with contradictory facts from one day to the next. On the Apple Watch path (deliberate recordings rather than all-day capture) the queue will be smaller; that is a labelled inference, and the day-1 falsification test measures how many pending facts one day of Watch recordings produces. |
| Severity | Low to medium for casual users: junk facts clutter the profile and the review chore is skipped (Forbes). High for agent users: the official Bee Skill tells agents to trust confirmed facts and treat unconfirmed ones as speculation, so a wrong fact confirmed by a hurried tap (employer, address, allergy, relationship) is injected into every agent session. Severity for agent users is inferred from the Skill's trust rule, not measured. |
| Current workaround | Tap through pending facts in the Bee app without seeing evidence (whether the app links a fact to its source conversation is not verified in reviewed sources); tell Bee's chat assistant a correction, which one App Store reviewer does; or ignore the queue, in which case agents see the facts as speculation. Agent users otherwise hand-write a user.md profile. |
| Amazon integration and its indispensable role | Bee is the only data source and the only write target: pending and confirmed facts (id, text, confirmed, created_at), verbatim speaker-labelled utterances, conversation start/end times and state, time-bounded keyword and neural search, the changefeed cursor, and the facts write API (confirm, update, delete, create). The exported trusted profile is built for consumers of Bee's Agent Skill and MCP. Amazon Bedrock (Nova Lite, Claude Haiku 4.5, us-east-1) runs the support/contradiction judge. No Echo, Fire TV or Ring. |
| Removal test (honest) | Remove Bee and there is nothing to audit: facts, utterances and speaker labels exist only in Bee and write-back has no target, so the product is undefined rather than degraded. The harder test is removing Hearsay: the Bee app already has confirm/dismiss, and an LLM with the Bee Skill can search transcripts on request. Our value is therefore limited to systematic per-fact evidence linking, speaker-source and media-like classification, contradiction and staleness detection, batch review, and the agent-ready trusted export. If the day-1 check shows the Bee app already displays per-fact source evidence (not verified either way in reviewed sources), the wedge narrows to speaker-source, contradiction and the export, and the pitch must say so. |
| Wow moment under 45 s | Split screen. Left: the iPhone Bee app's pending facts list with 9 items. Right: Hearsay's queue. Card 1: 'Sam has been a criminal lawyer for ten years' is tagged OTHER SPEAKER, ABOUT THEMSELVES, with the speaker_2 utterance quote from conversation 7731 and three clocks: spoken 14:02 (conversation start_time), fact created 14:31, fetched 14:40. Click 'Not about me, delete'; the terminal pane shows bee facts delete 9012 returning; the Bee app refreshes and the fact is gone. Card 2: 'Starts work at 7 am' is tagged WEARER SAID with the quote; click Confirm; it moves to Confirmed in the Bee app. Footer: 9 pending = 4 wearer-said, 3 other-said-about-self, 1 contradicts a confirmed fact, 1 no evidence found. Total about 40 seconds. |
| Original mechanism | A provenance ledger for machine-extracted memory. (1) Evidence retrieval: for each pending fact run bee search (keyword over conversations, then --neural) bounded by --since/--until = [created_at minus 48 h, created_at], pull candidate conversations with bee conversations get --json, and score utterances by lexical overlap and embedding similarity. (2) Support judge: a Bedrock structured-output call labels each candidate utterance supports / contradicts / unrelated and names the subject of the statement. (3) Speaker-source classification from Bee speaker labels: wearer-said (label equals the wearer's label chosen once at setup), other-said-about-wearer, other-said-about-self (misattribution), media-like (deterministic heuristic: a conversation with no wearer utterances, a single speaker label and a long monologue), none-found (phantom). (4) Contradiction and staleness: embed confirmed f… |
| Simulator or real-data route (data route) | Apple Watch Bee recording -> Bee cloud processing -> the user's Bee account -> Bee CLI 0.7.3 on the Mac (bee changed, bee facts, bee search, bee conversations get) -> local Node/TypeScript service with SQLite -> short utterance snippets (about 60 words max, non-wearer names masked) to Amazon Bedrock us-east-1 for judging -> local web UI on 127.0.0.1 -> write-back through the same CLI (bee facts confirm/update/delete/create) -> visible in the Bee app. No hosted endpoint; nothing leaves the Mac except the masked snippets sent for inference. |
| Authentication | bee login (browser approval link; Developer Mode by tapping the app version 5 times); the CLI's stored session is reused by every command; no OAuth or third-party app_id exists (forum 45413 unanswered). bee login with the post-23-Sep-2026 Amazon-account sign-in is undocumented. AWS: an IAM user or role with bedrock:InvokeModel in us-east-1 via local credentials. The local UI is unauthenticated and bound to localhost. |
| Preview constraints | Apple Watch recordings must be long: a participant reports short Watch recordings stuck in CAPTURING and later returning 404, and Amazon says recordings under about 50 words are deleted. Fact-extraction latency is undocumented, so the demo shows real clocks and may need a cut. Speaker labels are pseudonymous (speaker_1) unless named in the app, and the wearer may not be auto-identified (TechCrunch). bee changed bootstrap window is 10-24 h (docs disagree). bee sync 500s (issue #14), duplicate utterances (issue #3) and a macOS token-not-written login bug (issue #9) are reported. Whether Bee generates facts from Watch-only recordings, and whether the service works for an India-based account, are not verified. |
| Hardware | Required: an iPhone (iOS 17.6 or later) running Bee iOS app 1.2.154 with Developer Mode unlocked, paired with an Apple Watch (watchOS 10.0 or later) running the Bee Watch app as the recording source; an Apple-silicon Mac (M4 Pro, 24 GB) running the Bee CLI, the service and the UI. Not needed: Bee Pioneer (US-only shipping), Echo, Fire TV, Ring. People: one consenting adult friend for the misattribution scenario; optionally a self-recorded monologue by that friend played from a speaker for the media-like scenario. |
| SDK versions | @beeai/cli 0.7.3 (release 2026-06-27; last commit 2026-08-17 adds an undocumented external-access command), pinned in package.json. Node 25.2.1 on the Mac (the CLI's supported Node range is not stated in reviewed docs, unverified). @aws-sdk/client-bedrock-runtime v3 (exact version unverified). Bedrock models: Amazon Nova Lite and Claude Haiku 4.5 in us-east-1. SQLite via better-sqlite3 (version unverified). Bee iOS app 1.2.154 (Sep 19 2026), watchOS 10+. MCP is not required at runtime (CLI subprocess); optional bee mcp serve reportedly negotiates 2024-11-05 (entrant report, unverified). |
| Deployment prerequisites | Day 0-1 gate: the Bee account signs in from India and Developer Mode unlocks; bee login and bee status succeed; one Watch recording reaches COMPLETED and appears in bee changed; at least one pending fact appears within 24 h. AWS: a Paid-plan account with Bedrock quota above zero for Nova Lite and Haiku 4.5 in us-east-1, validated on day 1 (zero-quota and suspension reports in forum 45416/45384 with no escalation path); credits request by Oct 21 using the Devpost email. Repo: public GitHub with a detectable MIT LICENSE, README run section, redacted SIMULATED fixtures, product feedback and friction log. Optional: a Rule 11.6 clarification request on Watch-only accounts. |
| Firecrawl: research role | Used 3 of this round's 7 live Firecrawl commands: a scrape of the ChatGPT Memory FAQ and a search that surfaced Omi's Play listing and a YouTube review. Forbes, TechCrunch, App Store reviews and all Bee docs came from the Oct 3 saved scrapes, re-read today. |
| Firecrawl: runtime role | none |
| AWS Builder path | Bedrock does the only non-deterministic work: Nova Lite ($0.06/$0.24 per million tokens, Ohio table) labels supports/contradicts/unrelated and the statement subject in structured JSON; Claude Haiku 4.5 ($1/$5) adjudicates disagreements and contradictions. Bedrock batch inference re-runs the 40-item labelled set whenever the prompt changes so the README's accuracy number is reproducible. Build with Claude Code on Bedrock (forum 45321: counts like Kiro Crew) and describe each service in Product Feedback. Honest limit: the Bee CLI must run locally, so Lambda or AgentCore add little; serving the trusted profile from an AgentCore Runtime MCP resource is a stretch, not MVP. |
| Open Source path | Publish bee-evidence (MIT): a small TypeScript library that finds utterance-level evidence for a Bee fact (time-bounded search windows, candidate scoring, utterance dedupe for issue #3, a supports/contradicts result schema) with SIMULATED fixtures and CI, reusable by any Bee project. Contribution PR: bee-computer/bee-skill's README says MIT but the GitHub API reports license null (checked 2026-10-04), so a one-file LICENSE PR makes the licence detectable. Also file a reproducible bee-cli issue requesting a fact-to-source-conversation link in the facts payload. |
| Fallback | If facts are too sparse from Watch recordings, extend the same evidence mechanism to Bee todo suggestions (accept/dismiss write API) and say so. If bee search recall is poor, scan all conversations in the 48 h window via bee conversations list --json plus get. If speaker labels never distinguish the wearer, drop the wearer-said class and keep subject-of-statement, media-like and contradiction, stated honestly. If Bedrock quota fails, run the judge on a local model via Ollama and report the quality difference. If no capture path works by about day 5, the concept is ineligible: pivot to a non-Bee track. |
| Falsification test (≤1 day) | One day with the Watch: record three conversations longer than 5 minutes: (a) I state three facts about myself; (b) a consenting friend states three facts about themselves; (c) a self-recorded 4-minute monologue by the friend played from a speaker while I stay silent. Within 24 h run bee facts list --unconfirmed --json and the evidence pipeline. Pass: at least 4 pending facts appear; a supporting utterance is found for at least 70% of them; the source class is correct for at least 80% of those found (including at least one friend-stated fact that Bee attributed to me, or the classifier labelling it other-said-about-self). Fail: no pending facts from Watch recordings within 24 h (input too sparse), or evidence recall below 50%. |
| Risk profile | Lowest people- and consent-dependence of any Bee concept so far: a solo demo plus one friend, with other people's speech appearing only as short masked quotes that expire. Technical risk is moderate and concentrated in evidence recall and speaker labels. Idea risk is the largest component: utility perception and possible native overlap. Hardware and account risk are identical to every Bee concept (iPhone plus Watch path unverified from India). |
| Distinct from round 1 because | BEE-1..3 all took conversations as input to create new objects (two-party promises for households, retrieval prompts for students, attempt records for coding agents) and depended on a second surface or a coaching loop. BEE-4 takes Bee's facts object itself as the input, audits it with utterance evidence, and serves the wearer and the agents that consume Bee facts; there is no second platform, no counterparty, no spaced schedule and no code-symbol alignment. Its dominant risk is utility perception rather than two-person logistics (BEE-1), calendar spacing (BEE-2) or developer-behaviour adoptio… |
| Estimate (unmeasured) | Solo 90 h; two-person 52 h per person; cloud Estimate: $3-12 through 20 Nov 2026. Nova Lite: about 1,000 fact-utterance judgements x (1.5k in + 0.2k out) = about $0.14; Claude Haiku 4.5: about 300 adjudications x (4k in + 0.4k out) = about $1.80; embeddings small (Bedrock embedding price not verified this round); evaluation re-runs $1-5; everything else local ($0). Prices from the aws.amazon.com/bedrock/pricing Ohio table as recorded in aws.md; us-east-1 assumed equal.. Assumptions: Estimates, not measurements. Solo = one TypeScript developer at about 6.5 focused h/day over 16 build days (Oct 5-22 minus 2 reserve days) = about 104 h capacity, so 90 h leaves about 14 h buffer. Breakdown: setup (Developer Mode, CLI, Bedrock quota) 6; ingestion, cursor, dedupe 6; evidence retrieval 10; judge, source classifier, fixtures 12; media-like and staleness 5; contradiction 6; review UI 12; write-back and undo 5; trusted export 5; evaluation 6; real recording sessions 5; video, README… |

**Demand evidence** (quality labelled by the researcher):

- Forbes hands-on reviewer: every to-do and fact must be approved and the queue becomes unmanageable when behind; many facts were mistakenly pulled from TV shows, podcasts and other people, and facts contradicted each other day to day; recording via Apple Watch is possible. — [https://www.forbes.com/sites/forbes-personal-shopper/article/best-ai-wearables/](https://www.forbes.com/sites/forbes-personal-shopper/article/best-ai-wearables/) — *Hands-on journalism, single reviewer, affiliate links; byline 2026-07-24, og:updated 2026-09-16; saved copy read (retrieved 2026-10-03).*
- US App Store reviews of Bee report 'phantom' memories of things not spoken nearby, a detail about a conversation partner attributed to the wearer, and the device not always recognising the wearer's own voice. — [https://apps.apple.com/us/app/bee-your-personal-ai/id6480349491](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/bee-your-personal-ai/id6480349491) — *User reviews (anecdotes, undated; one reviewer admits using Bee's AI to write the review); saved copy read (retrieved 2026-10-03).*
- The official Bee Agent Skill instructs agents that unconfirmed facts are inferred and may be misinterpretations, to use them only as speculation with uncertainty flagged, and to trust confirmed facts; utterances are verbatim and authoritative while summaries may be inaccurate. — [https://github.com/bee-computer/bee-skill/blob/main/bee-cli/SKILL.md](https://github.com/bee-computer/bee-skill/blob/main/bee-cli/SKILL.md) — *Official repo (commit 2026-06-07); saved copy read (retrieved 2026-10-03). Establishes the trust gap, not user demand.*
- TechCrunch hands-on: transcripts can be messy, other speakers' names usually must be entered manually, and some sections of a conversation were omitted. — [https://techcrunch.com/2026/05/24/i-tried-amazons-bee-wearable-and-am-both-intrigued-and-slightly-creeped-out/](https://techcrunch.com/2026/05/24/i-tried-amazons-bee-wearable-and-am-both-intrigued-and-slightly-creeped-out/) — *Hands-on journalism, single reviewer, 2026-05-24; saved copy read (retrieved 2026-10-03).*
- OpenAI's Memory help page documents a 'Sources' panel showing which past chat, saved memory, file or connected email shaped personalization, with controls to correct or delete a saved memory and mark a source relevant or not: the provenance pattern is now expected for AI memory. — [https://help.openai.com/en/articles/8590148-memory-faq](https://help.openai.com/en/articles/8590148-memory-faq) — *Official vendor help page, shown as updated 14 days before 2026-10-04 (about 20 Sep 2026); read live 2026-10-04. Supply-side signal, not Bee user demand.*
- An Omi reviewer found the app's 'memories to review' were based on videos he had watched, the same media-contamination failure mode reported for Bee. — [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7b31iEYdRnk](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7b31iEYdRnk) — *Single YouTube review (uploaded 2025-11-13), auto-transcript excerpt; read live 2026-10-04; low to medium quality.*

**Rivals named at generation** (strongest feature; our difference; evidence status):

| Rival | Strongest feature | Our difference | Evidence status |
|---|---|---|---|
| [Bee native app (facts confirm/pending, chat corrections, Daily Insights)](https://www.aboutamazon.com/news/devices/bee-amazon-wearable-ai-device-new-features) | Facts and to-dos are extracted automatically and confirmed in-app; Actions, Daily Insights and Templates build on them; a reviewer could correct facts by telling the assistant. | Per-fact evidence with speaker-source and media-like classification, contradiction and staleness detection, batch review and an evidence-linked export for agents. | Vendor page (2026-01-05) and bee.computer homepage read (saved 2026-10-03). Whether the app shows a fact's source conversation is not verified in reviewed sources. |
| [ChatGPT Memory (OpenAI)](https://help.openai.com/en/articles/8590148-memory-faq) | A 'Sources' panel under responses shows which past chat, saved memory, custom instruction, file or email shaped personalization; users can correct or delete a saved memory and mark a source relevant or not. | Memory sourced from ambient spoken conversation with speaker attribution; detects misattributed and media-origin facts and contradictions; writes back into Bee; built for agent consumption of Bee facts. | Read live 2026-10-04 (page shown as updated about 20 Sep 2026). Availability varies by plan and region per the page. |
| [Omi](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.friend.ios&hl=en_US) | Auto-generated summaries, tasks and memories; Speech Profiles for speaker recognition; Brain Map; delete specific items; Apple Watch and pendant capture. | An audit layer with utterance-level evidence, speaker-source classes and contradiction checks rather than another memory store. | Google Play listing read live 2026-10-04 (updated 2026-10-03). Memory provenance or evidence links not verified in reviewed sources; a 2025 YouTube reviewer saw media-derived memories. |
| [Meta Muse](https://about.fb.com/news/2026/09/introducing-muse-personal-ai-agent/) | Remembers what matters to a person, lets them tell it to forget specific things, and shows an audit trail of actions done and planned. | Ambient speech as the source with speaker labels, evidence per fact, and write-back to Bee; Muse memory comes from the agent's own interactions. | Saved page read 2026-10-03 (published 2026-09-08, updated 2026-09-30). A per-memory evidence view is not verified in reviewed sources. |
| [Apple Siri Recap (watchOS 27 Audio Intelligence)](https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/09/siri-ai-a-profoundly-more-capable-and-personal-assistant-is-here/) | Produces a summary rather than a transcript and does not attribute speakers, with sensitive-data exclusion: privacy by omission. | The opposite design choice: keep attribution so the wearer can decide what to trust and delete; the contrast is a positioning risk as much as a difference. | Saved page read 2026-10-03 (2026-09-14; feature in beta later in 2026). |

**Smallest credible MVP:**

- Ingestion: bee changed --cursor --json every 60 s (cursor persisted only after processing) plus a bee facts list --unconfirmed --json bootstrap; conversations fetched with bee conversations get --json; utterance dedupe (bee-cli issue #3); SIMULATED-labelled fixtures for tests.
- Setup: the wearer picks their own speaker label from a recent conversation; label aliases stored locally; non-wearer names masked before any model call.
- Evidence retrieval: time-bounded bee search (keyword, then --neural) plus utterance scoring; candidate pointers (conversation id, utterance index, speaker label, spoken time) stored in SQLite.
- Judge: Bedrock Nova Lite structured output for supports/contradicts/unrelated plus statement subject; Claude Haiku 4.5 adjudicates disagreements; deterministic media-like heuristic; unit tests on 40 hand-labelled fact-utterance pairs.
- Contradiction and staleness against confirmed facts (embeddings plus LLM judge) with re-check dates for time-bound predicates.
- Review UI (local web, keyboard-driven): cards with quote, source class and three clocks; batch confirm / correct / delete / supersede via bee facts commands; undo log; stored quotes expire after 7 days.
- Export user.trusted.md and JSON with evidence pointers, plus a short Bee Skill snippet showing agents how to load it instead of raw facts.
- Evaluation and submission assets: at least 40 of my own pending facts hand-labelled; source-class accuracy and evidence recall reported in the README; friction log (missing fact-to-conversation link, Watch CAPTURING behaviour, bootstrap window); product feedback; MIT LICENSE.

**Deliberately excluded stretch:**

- AgentCore Runtime-hosted MCP resource serving the trusted profile to a teammate's agent
- Local-only judge model (Ollama) as a privacy option
- Automatic deletion without human review
- Voice-print speaker identification (no audio access exists)
- Editing Bee summaries or transcripts (no write API)
- Auditing Bee todo suggestions with the same mechanism
- Any Fire TV or Echo surface; multi-user accounts

**APIs, events and actions** (status as assessed by the researcher; 'unverified' means not confirmed in reviewed docs, 'gated' means not available to entrants, 'simulated' means not real in the demo):

| Status | API / event / action | Documentation |
|---|---|---|
| verified | bee facts list --unconfirmed / get / confirm / update --confirmed / delete / create / search (CLI) | https://docs.bee.computer/docs/cli |
| verified | bee search --query --filter conversations --since --until (keyword) and --neural (semantic) | https://docs.bee.computer/docs/cli |
| unverified | bee conversations get <id> --json and transcript <id> --since <epochMs> (per-utterance timestamp field not shown in CLI docs example) | https://docs.bee.computer/docs/cli |
| verified | bee changed --cursor --json (changefeed with meta.next_cursor) | https://docs.bee.computer/docs/agentic-sync |
| verified | bee stream --types new-utterance --json (SSE, at-most-once; payload has text, speaker, conversation_uuid only) | https://docs.bee.computer/docs/realtime |
| verified | MCP tools bee_list_facts, bee_update_fact, bee_delete_fact, bee_create_fact, bee_search (optional runtime path) | https://docs.bee.computer/docs/mcp |
| verified | Bee Agent Skill trust rule for confirmed vs unconfirmed facts (SKILL.md) | https://github.com/bee-computer/bee-skill/blob/main/bee-cli/SKILL.md |
| verified | Amazon Bedrock model availability by region (Nova Lite, Claude Haiku 4.5 in us-east-1) | https://docs.aws.amazon.com/bedrock/latest/userguide/models-regions.html |
| unverified | Amazon Bedrock Converse API (structured-output judge calls) | https://docs.aws.amazon.com/bedrock/latest/userguide/conversation-inference.html |
| unverified | Apple Watch Bee recording becomes a Bee conversation (CAPTURING then COMPLETED for longer recordings) | https://amazonappdev2026.devpost.com/forum_topics/45111-bee-developer-connect |

**Blockers:** Eligibility: without an iPhone plus Apple Watch running Bee (or a US-shipped Pioneer) no real data exists and the rules are not met; Watch capture parity and India service availability are unverified.; Input volume: whether Watch-only recordings generate enough pending facts for a convincing demo is unknown; a one-day test decides.; Evidence recall: if bee search misses the source utterance (window, transcription errors, omitted sections per TechCrunch) cards show 'no evidence' and look like false alarms.; Speaker labels: if the wearer is not reliably labelled, the wearer-said class collapses to subject-of-statement only.; Privacy: short quotes of other people's speech go to Bedrock even when masked; a judge may object.; Bedrock quota or suspension on a new AWS account (forum 45416, 45384) with no escalation path.; Native overlap: the Bee app may already show per-fact evidence (not verified); the Bee Skill lists contradiction detection as an example prompt.

**Reasons judges might reject it:** May read as a settings screen that Bee itself should ship (a utility, not a product), which hurts Quality of the Idea.; The Bee app may already show a fact's source conversation; this is not verified either way, so the wedge could shrink on day 1.; The Bee Skill's own example prompts include contradiction detection, so judges may call the core a latent LLM capability.; Demo volume on the Watch path may be thin (a handful of facts), making the review screen look sparse.; Privacy concern about sending other people's words to Bedrock, even masked and truncated.; The developer-experience framing (agent memory hygiene) is indirect, and the impact evidence is reviewer anecdotes rather than measurements.; No real-time element and no second device; the rules' Creative list names 'advance use of the facts and insights capabilities' but also cross-device and real-time examples that this concept skips.

**Gate result:** overall **conditional** (eligibility conditional, feasibility conditional; native overlap medium, visible crowding low).

- Eligibility: Same hard gate as BEE-1..3: Rules §4 require data 'recorded and processed through an actual Bee device or an Apple Watch running the Bee software', shown live in both the video and the code. The base-case user has no iPhone-plus-Apple-Watch and no Bee Pioneer (US-only shipping per the Bee Help Center). Forum 45263 ('no devices required... real (or even generated) audio') and the FAQ conflict with the rules, and the rules prevail (§11.4). Once a real capture path exists, the concept satisfies the runtime hook directly (bee facts, bee search, bee conversations, bee changed and the facts write API are called at runtime) and squarely targets the rules' Creative example 'advance use of the facts…
- Feasibility: The 90 h solo estimate fits the roughly 104 h capacity (estimate) with about 14 h of buffer, and no second platform or counterparty is involved. Verified from the saved CLI reference (updated 2026-06-07): bee facts list --unconfirmed / get / create / update --confirmed / confirm / delete / search; bee search with --neural and --since/--until (epoch ms); bee conversations get --json and transcript --since; bee changed --cursor; the new-utterance stream payload carries text, speaker and conversation_uuid with at-most-once delivery. The gating unknowns are input-side: whether Apple Watch-only recordings generate pending facts at all and in what volume (a participant in forum 45111 reports shor…
- Conditions: (1) Hardware path A: an iPhone (iOS 17.6+) plus an Apple Watch (watchOS 10+) running the Bee app with Developer Mode unlocked, whose recordings reach the CLI; or path B: a Bee Pioneer shipped to a US address and forwarded. In hand and working (bee login, bee status) from India by about day 5, or the concept is ineligible and abandoned. (2) The one-day falsification test passes: at least 4 pending facts appear within 24 h of three Watch recordings over 5 minutes each, a supporting utterance is found for at least 70% of them, and the source class is right for at least 80% of those found; otherwise extend to todo suggestions or pivot. (3) All recordings exceed 50 words; the consenting friend signs a release; non-wearer names are masked before any Bedrock call and stored quotes expire after 7 days; no real facts, transcripts or tokens are committed (redacted SIMULATED fixtures only). (4) Bedrock quota in us-east-1 is verified non-zero on day 1 on a Paid-plan account, or the judge runs on a local model with the quality difference reported. (5) Day-1 check of whether the Bee app already shows a fact's source conversation; if it does, the pitch narrows to speaker-source classification, contradiction and the agent-ready export and says so. (6) Pin @beeai/cli 0.7.3; the unreleased external-access command is not used; the Bee Skill's privacy guidance is followed. (7) Optional: a written Rule 11.6 clarification request on whether a Watch-only account qualifies.
- Native overlap: Bee natively extracts facts with a confirmed/pending state and lets users confirm or dismiss them in the app, and reviewers correct facts by telling the assistant (aboutamazon 2026-01-05; bee.computer; App Store reviews). The official Bee Skill's workflows already include building a user profile from history ('Deep Learning About the Owner') and list contradiction detection as an example prompt, so part of the thesis is a latent agent-plus-Skill capability. Per-fact utterance-level evidence, sp…
- Crowding: Bee remains the least crowded visible track: about 8-9 repos for 2 prizes (bee-cipher, beereflect-ai, ringfence, bee-bystander, ambient-guard, bellwether, kept, SansCue). None targets facts provenance; 'bee facts wearable created:>2026-08-31' returned 0 on 2026-10-04. Adjacent: bee-bystander (consent and redaction) and bee-cipher (privacy shield) share the privacy posture; developer-workflow Bee entries (ringfence, kept, Mental-Model-Drift) share the agent-memory audience. Lower bound: public r…
- Day-one proof: With hardware: Developer Mode is on and bee login plus bee status succeed from India. Record three Watch conversations longer than 5 minutes (self-facts, a consenting friend's facts, a 4-minute friend monologue played from a speaker). Within 24 h, bee facts list --unconfirmed --json returns at least 4 pending facts with created_at; bee search --since/--until (keyword, then --neural) over the 48 h window returns a conversation whose bee conversations get --json utterances contain a supporting line with a speaker label for at least 70% of them; bee facts delete <id> on one misattributed fact succeeds and the Bee app no longer shows it; a Bedrock Nova Lite Converse call in us-east-1 returns the structured supports/contradicts JSON (quota is not 0).
- Redesign to pass: If no Bee or Watch capture path works by about day 5, the concept is ineligible and should not be built; pivot to a non-Bee track. If hardware exists: run the day-1 app check and, if the Bee app already links facts to sources, lead with speaker-source classification, contradiction and staleness plus the evidence-linked user.trusted.md export for agents; reduce model dependence to Nova Lite only to limit Bedrock quota exposure; keep the bee-evidence library and the bee-skill LICENSE PR as the Op…

**Round-2 judge ratings (1–5; base case: solo, India, VVD on Mac, no Bee hardware, no US Ring device). Weighted = 0.25·Tech + 0.20·Design + 0.20·Impact + 0.20·Originality + 0.15·Feasibility.**

| Judge lens | Tech | Design | Impact | Originality | Feasibility | Weighted | Confidence | One-line verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| devrel | 1 | 3 | 2 | 3 | 1 | 2.00 | low | Lowest-logistics Bee idea and on-brief for facts, but a utility, and ineligible without a Watch. |
| engineer | 1 | 2 | 2 | 3 | 1 | 1.80 | low | A sound hygiene idea for Bee's facts layer, but ineligible without hardware, batch-and-desktop in a wearable track, and dependent on evidence recall that no source has demonstrated. |
| product | 1 | 3 | 3 | 3 | 1 | 2.20 | low | The most practical Bee concept if hardware appears: real reviewer-documented pain and deep facts-API use, but a small audience and a utility feel; ineligible in the base case. |
| **Mean of three** | 1.00 | 2.67 | 2.33 | 3.00 | 1.00 | **2.00** | | Scenario weighted: solo+hw 2.80; duo 2.00; duo+hw 3.12 |

**Why each judge rated it that way (verbatim rationale, one per criterion):**

- *devrel* — Tech: Ineligible in the base scenario (JDR-02): no Bee device or Watch. — Design: Keyboard-driven review with three clocks and batch write-back through the facts API is a coherent single-user tool, but it reads as a settings screen Bee should ship and has no second device or real-time element. — Impact: Evidence is reviewer anecdotes (Forbes, App Store) and the Skill's trust rule (JDR-22); agent-memory hygiene matters to a niche, and Watch-only fact volume may be thin. — Originality: Squarely targets 'advance use of the facts and insights capabilities' (JDR-03) and no visible Bee repo does facts provenance (JDR-25); but the Skill lists contradiction detection as an example prompt, ChatGPT's Sources panel (JDR-23) shows the pattern going native, and whether the Bee app already shows a fact's source is unverified. — Feasibility: No capture path.
  - Scenario adjustments: solo+hw tech 3, feasibility 3 (With iPhone+Watch the CLI calls are verified (facts, search, changed) and 90 h fits, but evidence recall, speaker labels and Watch fact generation are day-1 unknowns.); duo tech 1, feasibility 1 (Still ineligible.); duo+hw tech 4, feasibility 4 (52 h each; evaluation set and export both land.)
- *engineer* — Tech: No iPhone-plus-Watch and no Pioneer in the base case, so there are no real pending facts to audit and the Bee rule (evt-rules.md L348) fails at Stage 1. Merit scores below assume it is built. — Design: A keyboard-driven local web review queue with quote, source class and three clocks is a complete design for the job, but in a wearable track it is a desktop batch utility: no wrist, no second device, no real-time element, and the rules' Creative list for Bee emphasises cross-device, real-time and on-wrist experiences. It may read as a settings screen Bee should ship. — Impact: Reviewer anecdotes (Forbes byline 24 Jul 2026, App Store reviews, TechCrunch) establish junk and misattributed facts, and the official Bee Skill's trust rule makes wrong confirmed facts consequential for agent users, but that audience is an unmeasured minority and the Watch path may yield few pending facts per day (the concept's own falsification test). — Originality: No facts-provenance project among the six saved Bee repos ('bee facts wearable' = 0 on Oct 4), and the saved CLI reference's bee facts get example shows only timezone, created_at, confirmed and text with no source-conversation pointer (.firecrawl/bee-cli-commands.md), which supports the premise. Offsets: the official Bee Skill already lists contradiction detection as an example prompt, ChatGPT Memory's Sources panel… — Feasibility: Ineligible without a capture path; the gate says abandon by about day 5 if none works.
  - Scenario adjustments: solo+hw tech 3, feasibility 3 (With iPhone plus Watch, 90 h fits about 104 h (estimates) with no second person. Tech capped at 3 because the core judgement is a Bedrock LLM call over utterances found by bee search (recall unproven), speaker-source cl…); duo tech 1, feasibility 1 (Still ineligible without Bee hardware.); duo+hw tech 4, feasibility 4 (At about 52 h each (estimate) the 40-item labelled evaluation, contradiction engine and export all land, lifting tech to 4 on measured accuracy; the input-volume unknown remains.)
- *product* — Tech: No Bee device or Apple Watch in the base scenario; ineligible under Rules L348 (evt-rules.md). — Design: Keyboard-driven local review queue with the quote, speaker-source class and three honest clocks (spoken, processed, fetched), batch confirm/correct/delete through Bee's facts write API, and an evidence-linked trusted export for agents (concept mechanism). Appropriate for the wearer's desk, but it is a settings-style audit tool rather than an on-wrist or ambient experience, and the review screen may look sparse on Wa… — Impact: The pain is documented by independent reviewers: Forbes (byline 24 Jul 2026, updated 16 Sep 2026) reports facts pulled from TV and podcasts, contradictions day to day and an unmanageable approval queue; App Store reviews report phantom and misattributed memories (trends_agents.md T5; concept demand_evidence). ChatGPT Memory now exposes a Sources panel for correcting or deleting memories (bee2-chatgpt-memory-faq.md, … — Originality: Bee already has confirmed/pending facts with confirm/dismiss and the official Skill lists contradiction detection as an example prompt (gate native_overlap medium); whether the app shows a fact's source conversation is unverified either way, so the wedge could shrink on day 1. Per-fact utterance evidence, speaker-source and media-like classification, staleness dates and the trusted export are not verified in Bee, Om… — Feasibility: Ineligible without hardware; with hardware 90 h fits with about 14 h buffer and needs only one consenting friend, but Watch-only fact volume and bee search recall are unknown (gate conditions).
  - Scenario adjustments: solo+hw tech 3, feasibility 3 (iPhone plus Watch makes it eligible and the facts write API is deep use of the platform ('advance use of facts' Creative item), but Watch-only pending-fact volume is unverified, so tech capped at 3; 90 h with a one-day …); duo tech 1, feasibility 1 (Ineligible without real Bee data.); duo+hw tech 3, feasibility 4 (52 h per person; lowest people dependence of the Bee set.)

**Red-team verdicts:** not red-teamed (did not reach the top of either round's ranking); ranking rests on gate and judge scores alone.

---

#### BEE-5: Asked Again: a recurring-question bank for tutors and TAs, built from Bee-recorded doubt sessions, with live 'you answered this before' prompts

**Round:** 2. **Track:** Bee (primary). Priority use case: education (tutor and TA side), with real-time functionality and 'synced insights across sessions' as the creative hooks. Mini challenges: AWS Builder (Bedrock extraction, embeddings and coverage judge; built with Claude Code on Bedrock) and Open Source (asked-again Agent Skill plus a docs PR to bee-cli). No Fire TV, Echo or Ring surface.

**Pitch:** Tutors, TAs and coaching-centre teachers answer the same doubts across sections and weeks, and the spoken answers are never captured, so answers drift and the FAQ never gets written. Asked Again treats each Apple Watch Bee recording of a doubt session as one session: Bee's speaker labels separate student questions from the tutor's answers, student identities are discarded at ingestion, questions are clustered across sessions ('asked 4 times in 3 sessions'), checked against the tutor's own notes for coverage gaps, and turned into a draft doubt sheet in the tutor's own words. During a session a best-effort live mode shows the tutor their previous answer when a recurring doubt is asked again, and Bee to-dos remind them to publish the FAQ before the next session.

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| Primary user | Tutors, university teaching assistants and coaching-centre teachers who run recurring small-group doubt-clearing sessions or office hours for the same course over a term. First segments (assumption): Indian coaching 'doubt sessions' and university CS office hours. The tutor is the wearer and announces recording; students are adults who consent per session. The demo uses two consenting adult friends as students across at least three sessions. |
| Frequency | Every doubt session. One university's open-source office-hours queue facilitated more than 70,000 questions across 25 courses and 4 advising offices since Fall 2017 (Mussulman et al., ASEE 2020), and office-hour demand in CS1 courses spikes near deadlines with long waits and overworked staff (arXiv 2608.06753, posted Aug 2026; SIGCSE 2024 paper). How many in-person doubts are repeats is not measured in reviewed sources; the falsification test measures it on my own sessions (labelled inference). |
| Severity | Medium for the tutor: repeated explanations and inconsistent answers cost preparation and session time, and TA time is capped (a UCSB handbook caps TA duties at 20 h/week including office hours), so repeats compete with grading. Medium to high for students near deadlines (long waits per the office-hours studies). The specific cost of repeated questions is inferred, not measured. |
| Current workaround | Typed forums such as Piazza and Ed Discussion (students must type; Piazza tells instructors to use posted questions as a gauge of confusing topics); the tutor's memory and ad-hoc notes; Otter-style recordings that produce summaries, not a cross-session question bank; TeachFX, which reports talk time and question counts but not question content. In-person spoken doubts are mostly lost. |
| Amazon integration and its indispensable role | Bee provides the session boundary (one Watch recording becomes one conversation with start/end time and state), verbatim speaker-labelled utterances to separate student questions from tutor answers, time-bounded neural search across past sessions to confirm 'asked before', the changefeed cursor for reliable ingestion, the realtime stream and transcript polling for live mode, and todo write-back with alarms for FAQ reminders on the tutor's phone. Amazon Bedrock runs extraction, embeddings and the coverage judge. No Echo, Fire TV or Ring. |
| Removal test (honest) | Without Bee, a phone recorder plus Whisper and diarisation could produce similar raw material, but nothing would be hands-free, there would be no cross-session store or neural search to query, no speaker labels aligned with that store, no reminder path to the tutor's phone, and no event source for live mode; the tutor would have to run a recording app on the table and build all of that. Bee is indispensable as the capture, labelling, search and write-back substrate; the clustering, coverage check and doubt sheet are ours. Honest weakness: if speaker labels fail to separate the tutor from students, role assignment degrades to heuristics (interrogatives, answer length) and the Bee dependence weakens to capture plus search. |
| Wow moment under 45 s | The tutor (Watch on the wrist, Bee recording indicator shown) is mid-session with two consenting friends. One asks: 'Why does binary search need the array to be sorted first?' A clock overlay starts at the question; within seconds of Bee emitting the utterance the tutor's laptop shows a card: 'Asked 3 times before (Sep 28, Oct 1, Oct 2). Your previous answer, Oct 1: [quote]. Not covered in your notes (closest: Unit 3, slide 12).' The card carries spoken / processed / fetched clocks. The tutor answers consistently. Cut to after the session (an on-screen clock shows the real processing delay): the dashboard cluster count reads 4 and the iPhone Bee app shows a new todo 'Add to FAQ: why sorted input for binary search (asked 4x)' with an alarm one hour before the next session. About 40 seconds. |
| Original mechanism | A cross-session question ledger with role separation and coverage checking. (1) Session = one Bee conversation (Watch start/stop). Role mapping: the wearer's speaker label is set once; every other label is 'student' and is discarded at ingestion so only question text survives (privacy by construction). (2) Q/A pair extraction with Bedrock structured output over the utterance sequence: a question span by a non-wearer followed by the wearer's answer span, each with conversation id, utterance index, spoken time (or conversation start_time) and a verbatim quote of 25 words or fewer. (3) Clustering: Bedrock embeddings plus an agglomerative threshold produce clusters with per-session counts and first/last asked dates; bee search --neural --since confirms matches in conversations our store may have missed. (4) Coverage check: the tutor's notes (markdown or PDF) are chunked and embedded; an LLM… |
| Simulator or real-data route (data route) | Apple Watch Bee recording started and stopped by the tutor -> Bee processing -> Bee CLI on the Mac (bee changed, bee conversations get/transcript, bee search --neural, bee stream) -> local TypeScript service with SQLite (questions, clusters, tutor answers; no student labels) -> Amazon Bedrock us-east-1 for extraction, embeddings and the coverage judge (student names masked; quotes of 25 words or fewer) -> local dashboard and live card on 127.0.0.1 -> bee todos create write-back -> the tutor's iPhone (and Watch if Bee mirrors alarms there; unverified). No hosted endpoint. |
| Authentication | bee login with Developer Mode (tap the app version 5 times); the CLI session is reused by every command; AWS IAM credentials for bedrock:InvokeModel in us-east-1; the local dashboard is unauthenticated on localhost. Consent: a verbal announcement at the start of every session plus a signed one-page form for demo participants; no minors are recorded; recordings outside the demo are the tutor's responsibility under local law. |
| Preview constraints | Watch recordings must be long enough to reach COMPLETED (short ones stall in CAPTURING and vanish per forum 45111; recordings under about 50 words are deleted). Live mode depends on bee stream, which is at-most-once, carries no utterance timestamps and reportedly dies after about 43 h, so latency and misses are shown honestly and the transcript --since poll is the backstop. Speaker labels may not separate the tutor reliably (TechCrunch: names often entered by hand), forcing the role-heuristic fallback. Processing latency is undocumented. Hindi or Hinglish transcription quality is unverified. Service availability for an India-based Bee account is unverified. |
| Hardware | Required: an iPhone (iOS 17.6 or later) running Bee iOS app 1.2.154 with Developer Mode unlocked, paired with an Apple Watch (watchOS 10.0 or later) running the Bee Watch app as the recording source; an Apple-silicon Mac (M4 Pro, 24 GB) running the Bee CLI, the service and the dashboard. Not needed: Bee Pioneer (US-only shipping), Echo, Fire TV, Ring. People: two consenting adult friends acting as students across at least three sessions on different days. Materials: the tutor's notes as PDF or markdown. |
| SDK versions | @beeai/cli 0.7.3 (release 2026-06-27; last commit 2026-08-17), pinned. Node 25.2.1 on the Mac (CLI Node range not stated in docs, unverified). @aws-sdk/client-bedrock-runtime v3 (exact version unverified). Bedrock models: Nova Lite (extraction), Claude Haiku 4.5 (coverage judge), Titan Text Embeddings v2 (unverified this round). SQLite via better-sqlite3 and a PDF text extractor such as pdf-parse (versions unverified). Bee iOS app 1.2.154, watchOS 10+. MCP optional at runtime; the Skill is packaged in the same SKILL.md format Bee uses. |
| Deployment prerequisites | Day 0-1 gate: the Bee account works from India with Developer Mode; bee login and bee status succeed; one 20-minute Watch session reaches COMPLETED with at least two distinct speaker labels and appears in bee changed. AWS: Paid-plan account with Bedrock quota above zero validated on day 1; credits request by Oct 21 with the Devpost email. Calendar: real sessions must start by about Oct 8 so at least three sessions exist before the video. Consent forms signed by both demo participants. Repo: public GitHub with a detectable MIT LICENSE, README run section, SIMULATED fixtures, product feedback and friction log. |
| Firecrawl: research role | Used 4 of this round's 7 live Firecrawl commands: scrapes of the Piazza overview and the Otter Education page, a search that surfaced TeachFX and the Edthena comparison, and a research-filtered search that surfaced the UIUC Queue paper (ASEE 2020), arXiv 2608.06753 and two SIGCSE/CSEDM office-hours papers. A plain web search for office-hours anecdotes was low-yield (one Stack Exchange thread, a TA handbook). |
| Firecrawl: runtime role | none (a stretch could let the tutor paste a public course-page URL to build the materials corpus with firecrawl scrape, but notes are usually local PDFs or slides, so it is excluded) |
| AWS Builder path | Bedrock Nova Lite extracts Q/A pairs as structured JSON over each session; Titan Text Embeddings cluster questions and index the tutor's notes (price and version to confirm on day 1); Claude Haiku 4.5 judges coverage with citations into the notes. Bedrock batch inference re-runs the labelled session set for a reproducible precision/recall number in the README. Build with Claude Code on Bedrock (forum 45321). Optional: a Strands agent wraps extraction, clustering and coverage as tools for the doubt-sheet step and records traces. Honest limit: the Bee CLI runs locally, so no AgentCore Runtime or Lambda; AWS depth is model hosting plus a reproducible evaluation. |
| Open Source path | Publish asked-again as an installable Agent Skill plus CLI (MIT): the role-separated Q/A extraction prompt and schema, cross-session clustering, the coverage-check prompt, and SIMULATED fixture sessions with CI, reusable for any Bee session-analysis project (standups, interviews, clinics). Contribution: a docs PR to bee-cli's README documenting the observed Apple Watch CAPTURING-then-disappearance behaviour and the snapshot-on-first-observe pattern, after reproducing it; the friction log doubles as the PR body. |
| Fallback | If speaker labels fail, assign roles by heuristics (interrogatives, answer length, the wearer labelled once) and state the degradation. If the stream is unusable, drop live mode and present post-session recurrence and coverage only (still Bee-dependent via changefeed, search and todos). If friends cannot attend three sessions, run shorter sessions on consecutive days. If Bedrock fails, use local embeddings and a local LLM with lower quality reported. If no capture path works by about day 5, the concept is ineligible: pivot to a non-Bee track. |
| Falsification test (≤1 day) | One day: run two 15-20-minute consented mock doubt sessions on the Watch with a friend asking 8 scripted questions each, 3 of which repeat across the two sessions. Pass: both conversations reach COMPLETED with at least two speaker labels; at least 6 of 8 questions per session are extracted as Q/A pairs with the right role; at least 2 of the 3 repeats cluster together (or match via bee search --neural); a bee todos create --alarm-at reminder reaches the iPhone. Fail: labels do not separate tutor from student (role accuracy below 70%) or the recordings never complete. |
| Risk profile | People- and consent-dependent (at least two consenting participants across at least three sessions before the video) with moderate technical risk concentrated in speaker roles and the lossy stream. Visible crowding is low: no education entrant among visible Bee repos and no tutor-side tool among reviewed rivals. Scope sits at the 90 h limit because of live mode, which is the first thing to cut. Hardware and account risk are identical to every Bee concept. |
| Distinct from round 1 because | The user is the teacher or tutor, not the student (BEE-2), a household (BEE-1) or a developer handing work to an agent (BEE-3). The unit of work is a recurring multi-speaker session rather than a promise, a spaced prompt or a debugging attempt. The mechanism is role-separated question mining, cross-session clustering, materials coverage and live prior-answer recall: no two-key state machine, no grading of the wearer's answers, no code-symbol alignment. The risk is consent and people logistics plus stream reliability, not revision linking, Leitner spacing or identifier alignment. It also diffe… |
| Estimate (unmeasured) | Solo 90 h; two-person 52 h per person; cloud Estimate: $3-12 through 20 Nov 2026. Nova Lite extraction over about 30 sessions x (40k in + 3k out) = about $0.10; Claude Haiku 4.5 coverage judging about 300 x (5k in + 0.3k out) = about $2.00; embeddings for about 2,000 chunks and questions small (Titan price not verified this round); evaluation re-runs $1-5; local hosting $0. Prices from the Bedrock pricing Ohio table as recorded in aws.md; us-east-1 assumed equal.. Assumptions: Estimates, not measurements. Solo capacity about 104 h (6.5 focused h/day x 16 build days, Oct 5-22 minus 2 reserve); 90 h leaves about 14 h buffer. Breakdown: setup 6; ingestion, snapshot, dedupe 8; role mapping and privacy 3; Q/A extractor and tests 10; clustering and neural cross-check 8; coverage check 10; doubt sheet and todo write-back 6; live mode 10; dashboard 7; evaluation 6; real sessions (at least 3 with friends) 6; video, README, friction log, feedback 10. Duo = hours per person whe… |

**Demand evidence** (quality labelled by the researcher):

- An open-source office-hours queue at one university was adopted by 25 courses and 4 advising offices and facilitated more than 70,000 questions since Fall 2017; the authors note students can see that others have the same question. — [https://www.researchgate.net/publication/344699426_Measuring_Impact_Student_and_Instructor_Experience_Using_an_Online_Queue](https://www.researchgate.net/publication/344699426_Measuring_Impact_Student_and_Instructor_Experience_Using_an_Online_Queue) — *Peer-reviewed conference paper (ASEE 2020, June 2020) via ResearchGate copy; read live 2026-10-04. One institution; volume evidence, not evidence about repeats.*
- Office-hour utilization in CS courses spikes near deadlines, producing long wait times, frustrated students and overworked staff; a split-deadline policy increased office-hour efficiency in a large CS1 course. — [https://arxiv.org/html/2608.06753v1](https://arxiv.org/html/2608.06753v1) — *Peer-reviewed (SIGCSE 2024 proceedings; arXiv v1 posted 2026-08-07); read live 2026-10-04. One course.*
- Piazza markets its typed Q&A to instructors as a gauge of which topics and assignments confuse students, with anonymity options and participation reports; in-person spoken questions are outside its scope. — [https://piazza.com/product/overview](https://piazza.com/product/overview) — *Vendor marketing, undated; read live 2026-10-04.*
- TeachFX sells audio recording of lessons that yields teacher/student/group talk time, open-ended question counts, specific praise and wait time, showing teachers already record sessions for reflection; question content is not banked. — [https://teachfx.com/](https://teachfx.com/) — *Vendor marketing, undated (sample report dated 2026-02-24); read live 2026-10-04; corroborated by a competitor page (Edthena, updated about Jul 2026) with conflict of interest.*
- TA workload at one university is capped at 20 hours per week including office hours, section prep, grading and email, so time spent re-answering questions competes with other duties. — [https://carter.faculty.pstat.ucsb.edu/TAHandbook/Part_1_TA_Expectations/workload.html](https://carter.faculty.pstat.ucsb.edu/TAHandbook/Part_1_TA_Expectations/workload.html) — *Official departmental handbook, undated; read live 2026-10-04. Context, not direct demand.*

**Rivals named at generation** (strongest feature; our difference; evidence status):

| Rival | Strongest feature | Our difference | Evidence status |
|---|---|---|---|
| [Piazza](https://piazza.com/product/overview) | Wiki-style Q&A with a single students' answer and a single instructors' answer, anonymity options, instructor endorsements, and participation and class reports instructors use to see where students struggle. | Captures spoken in-person doubts hands-free, clusters repeats across sessions, checks the tutor's own materials for gaps, and drafts answers from the tutor's spoken words; Piazza requires typing. | Read live 2026-10-04 (undated page). Duplicate or similar-question detection is not verified in reviewed sources. |
| [TeachFX](https://teachfx.com/) | Records a lesson on a phone or laptop and reports teacher, student and group talk time, silence, open-ended question counts, specific praise and wait time; teachers own recordings and set goals. | Question content and recurrence rather than discourse metrics; coverage against materials; live prior-answer recall; Bee to-do reminders. | Homepage read live 2026-10-04; feature list corroborated by a competitor page (Edthena). A question bank or FAQ output is not verified in reviewed sources. |
| [Otter Education Notetaker](https://otter.ai/education-agent) | Turns lectures into structured summaries, customizable study guides and targeted study questions generated from a syllabus or notes, and compares lecture notes with textbook content to fill gaps. | Tutor-side rather than student-side; cross-session recurrence of student questions; live consistency prompts; wearable capture through Bee. | Read live 2026-10-04 (undated page). An instructor-side recurring-question bank is not verified in reviewed sources. |
| [Bee native Templates and conversation summaries](https://www.aboutamazon.com/news/devices/bee-amazon-wearable-ai-device-new-features) | Templates recap a single session into actionable next steps (for example a lecture into a study plan) inside the Bee app. | Multi-session aggregation and role-separated question mining with coverage checks; Templates work per conversation. | Saved page (2026-01-05) read 2026-10-03. The template catalogue itself was not reviewed. |
| [Granola](https://www.granola.ai/) | Meeting notes without a bot, enhanced notes, and chat across meetings including 'what did I promise' style questions, with an MCP connector. | In-person small-group teaching sessions, student-question recurrence and materials coverage rather than meeting notes. | Saved page read 2026-10-03 (undated). Cross-session question clustering is not verified in reviewed sources. |

**Smallest credible MVP:**

- Ingestion daemon: bee changed --cursor --json (cursor persisted after processing) fetching COMPLETED conversations with bee conversations get --json; transcripts snapshotted on first observation because Watch recordings can later disappear (forum 45111); utterance dedupe; SIMULATED fixture sessions for tests.
- Role mapping and privacy: the wearer's label set once; non-wearer labels discarded; only question text, tutor answer, timestamps and a per-session consent flag are stored.
- Q/A pair extractor (Bedrock Nova Lite structured output) with unit tests on fixture transcripts; precision and recall measured on my own sessions and reported.
- Clustering with recurrence counts and first/last asked dates, cross-checked with bee search --neural; cluster view in the dashboard.
- Materials coverage: ingest the tutor's notes (markdown or PDF via a local parser), embed, and LLM-judge covered / partial / not covered with a citation.
- Doubt sheet export (markdown) plus bee todos create --alarm-at write-back for 'Add to FAQ' items; completion read back via bee changed.
- Live mode: bee stream plus transcript polling, cluster match, prior-answer card with latency clocks; automatic degrade to post-session mode if the stream drops.
- Local dashboard (sessions, clusters, gaps) and submission assets: README with a verified-vs-simulated table, friction log (Watch CAPTURING behaviour, at-most-once stream), product feedback, MIT LICENSE.

**Deliberately excluded stretch:**

- Student-facing answers or any chatbot
- Identifying individual students or tracking who asked
- Grading or assessment of students
- Publishing to an LMS, Piazza or Ed Discussion
- Fire TV or Echo surfaces
- Multi-tutor sync across a teaching team ('synced insights across teams')
- Automatic FAQ publishing without tutor edit
- Hindi or Hinglish sessions (transcription quality unverified); the demo is in English
- Optional Firecrawl ingestion of a public course page as materials
